// src/components/features/CarParts/CarPartsContent.tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { ShoppingBag, Car as CarIcon, Settings, Search, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useSearchFilters } from '@/shared/hooks/useSearchFilters';
import { useGetCarPage, useGetCarListFlat } from '@/domains/front/reference/car/hooks/car.hooks';
import { useGetPartCategoryPage } from '@/domains/front/part/hooks/part.hooks';
import { useSearchProducts } from '@/domains/front/product/hooks/product.hooks';
import { SearchSidebar } from '@/components/features/ProductGrid/SearchSidebar';
import { ProductSearchCard } from '@/components/features/ProductCard/ProductSearchCard';
import { ProductCardSkeleton } from '@/components/features/ProductCard/ProductCardSkeleton';
import { Pagination } from '@/components/composites/Pagination/Pagination';
import { Breadcrumb } from '@/components/composites/Breadcrumb/Breadcrumb';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { Input } from '@/components/primitives/Input/Input';
import { Typography } from '@/components/primitives/Typography';
import { useAppStore } from '@/shared/store/useAppStore';
import { SortSelector } from '@/components/composites/SortSelector/SortSelector';
import { ShopProductAdBanner } from '@/components/features/ProductCard/ShopProductAdBanner';
import { Modal, ModalBody } from '@/components/composites/Modal/Modal';
import { ChangeCategoryModal } from '@/components/features/Part/components/ChangeCategoryModal';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';
import { getCarUrl, getFullUrl } from '@/core/utils/formatters';
import { PageDescription } from '@/components/composites/PageDescription/PageDescription';

export interface CarPartsContentProps {
  partSlug: string;
  carSlug: string;
}

export function CarPartsContent({ partSlug, carSlug }: CarPartsContentProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isMobileFiltersOpen, setIsMobileFiltersOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [isChangeCarOpen, setIsChangeCarOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [isChangeCatOpen, setIsChangeCatOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = React.useState('');

  const decodedCarModel = decodeURIComponent(carSlug).replace(/-/g, ' ');

  const { filters, setFilter, clearFilters } = useSearchFilters();
  const currentFilters = {
    ...filters,
    carModel: decodedCarModel,
    partCategoryEnglishTitle: partSlug,
  };

  const { data: carData } = useGetCarPage(decodedCarModel);
  const { data: categoryData } = useGetPartCategoryPage(partSlug);
  const { data: productsResponse, isLoading: isProductsLoading } = useSearchProducts(currentFilters);
  const { data: cars = [], isLoading: isCarsLoading } = useGetCarListFlat(1, 200);

  const products = productsResponse?.items || [];
  const totalCount = productsResponse?.totalCount || 0;
  const totalPages = productsResponse?.totalPages || 1;

  // استخراج دیتای تجمیعی برای فیلترهای پویا
  const aggregations = (productsResponse as any)?.aggregations || null;

  const breadcrumbItems = [
    { id: 'cars-root', title: 'خودروها', href: '/search' },
    { id: 'car-active', title: carData?.model || decodedCarModel, href: getCarUrl(carSlug) },
    { id: 'part-active', title: categoryData?.category?.name || partSlug },
  ];

  const filteredCars = cars.filter((car: any) =>
    car.model.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4 sm:gap-6" dir="rtl">
      
      {/* هدر چسبان موبایل با همان ابعاد و کلاس‌های کامپکت و دقیق مدنظر شما */}
      <div
        className="sticky top-14 z-30 -mx-4 -mt-2 flex w-[calc(100%+2rem)] shrink-0 select-none flex-col gap-1 border-b border-border/80 bg-card/95 px-3 py-2 shadow-xs backdrop-blur-md transition-all duration-normal md:hidden"
      >
        <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between mt-2">
          <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-border/70 bg-muted/40 p-0.5">
              <img src={getFullUrl(carData?.cover)} className="h-full w-full object-contain" alt="" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col">
              <span className="text-[0.625rem] font-bold text-muted-foreground leading-none">
                {categoryData?.category?.name || 'گروه قطعات'}
              </span>
              <span className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs font-bold text-foreground">
                {carData?.model || 'خودرو'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setSearchQuery('');
                setIsChangeCarOpen(true);
              }}
              className="rounded-md bg-accent px-2 py-1 text-3xs font-bold text-accent-foreground active:scale-95 transition-transform"
            >
              تغییر خودرو
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsChangeCatOpen(true)}
              className="rounded-md bg-secondary px-2 py-1 text-3xs font-bold text-secondary-foreground active:scale-95 transition-transform"
            >
              تغییر قطعه
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ردیف دوم: فیلترها و مرتب‌سازی کامپکت */}
        <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-dashed border-border/60 pt-1.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsMobileFiltersOpen(true)}
            className="flex h-8 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-input bg-card px-2.5 text-2xs font-bold shadow-2xs active:scale-95 transition-transform"
          >
            <span>فیلترها</span>
          </button>

          <SortSelector
            value={filters.orderType || 'Selected'}
            onChange={(val) => setFilter('sort', val)}
            variant="trigger"
          />

          <span className="flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-muted px-2 text-3xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
            {new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR').format(totalCount)} کالا
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Breadcrumb items={breadcrumbItems} className="hidden px-4 md:flex md:px-0" />

      {/* کارت دسکتاپ هدر مشخصات خودرو و قطعه */}
      <div className="hidden md:block">
        <Card className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-5 p-4 shadow-xs rounded-2xl border-border/80">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
              <div className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/70 bg-muted/30 p-1">
                <img src={getFullUrl(carData?.cover)} className="h-full w-full object-contain" alt="" />
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                <span className="text-label-2xs">خودرو انتخابی:</span>
                <h2 className="text-title-sm md:text-base">
                  {carData?.model || decodedCarModel}
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="h-10 w-px bg-border" />

            <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
              <div className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/70 bg-muted/30 p-2">
                {categoryData?.category?.thumbnail ? (
                  <img
                    src={getFullUrl(categoryData.category.thumbnail)}
                    className="h-full w-full object-contain filter dark:invert"
                    alt=""
                  />
                ) : (
                  <Settings aria-hidden="true" className="h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground" />
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                <span className="text-label-2xs">دسته قطعات:</span>
                <h2 className="text-title-sm md:text-base">
                  {categoryData?.category?.name || partSlug}
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => {
                setSearchQuery('');
                setIsChangeCarOpen(true);
              }}
              className="h-10 px-5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl"
            >
              تغییر خودرو
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="primary"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => setIsChangeCatOpen(true)}
              className="h-10 px-5 text-xs font-bold shadow-xs rounded-xl"
            >
              تغییر قطعه
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full items-start gap-6 md:gap-8">
        <SearchSidebar
          filters={currentFilters}
          aggregations={aggregations}
          onFilterChange={(name, val) => setFilter(name, val)}
          onClearAll={clearFilters}
          isOpen={isMobileFiltersOpen}
          onClose={() => setIsMobileFiltersOpen(false)}
          carId={carData?.id}
          partCategoryId={categoryData?.category?.id}
        />

        {/* کانتینر محصولات */}
        <div className="flex flex-1 min-w-0 flex-col items-stretch gap-5">
          <div className="w-full">
            <ShopProductAdBanner carId={carData?.id} partCategoryId={categoryData?.category?.id} />
          </div>

          <div className="hidden md:block">
            <SortSelector
              value={filters.orderType || 'Selected'}
              onChange={(val) => setFilter('sort', val)}
              className="mt-1"
            />
          </div>

          {isProductsLoading ? (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 2xl:grid-cols-4">
              {Array.from({ length: 10 }).map((_, i) => (
                <ProductCardSkeleton key={i} />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : products.length > 0 ? (
            <>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 2xl:grid-cols-4">
                {products.map((prod) => (
                  <ProductSearchCard key={prod.id} product={prod} />
                ))}
              </div>

              <Pagination
                currentPage={filters.pageNumber || 1}
                totalPages={totalPages}
                onPageChange={(page) => setFilter('page', page)}
              />
            </>
          ) : (
            <div className="mx-4 flex select-none flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/80 bg-card py-16 text-center md:mx-0 shadow-xs">
              <ShoppingBag aria-hidden="true" className="mb-4 h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground/40" />
              <Typography variant="h4" className="font-bold text-foreground">
                کالایی یافت نشد
              </Typography>
              <p className="mt-2 text-base text-muted-foreground">
                هنوز هیچ محصول فعال و موجودی برای این ترکیب خودرو و قطعه یافت نشد.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {categoryData?.category?.description ? (
        <PageDescription
          htmlContent={categoryData.category.description}
          title={`راهنمای خرید و شناخت قطعات ${categoryData.category.name} مخصوص ${carData?.model || decodedCarModel}`}
        />
      ) : null}

      {/* مودال انتخاب خودرو */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={isChangeCarOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsChangeCarOpen(false)}
        className="flex h-full max-h-full w-full max-w-none flex-col rounded-none bg-card p-0 md:relative md:h-[34.375rem] md:max-h-[90vh] md:max-w-2xl md:rounded-2xl md:overflow-hidden"
        overlayClassName="bg-foreground/40 backdrop-blur-md"
      >
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b border-border bg-muted/30 px-4 py-4">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-title-sm">
            <CarIcon aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
            تغییر خودرو فعال
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsChangeCarOpen(false)}
            aria-label="بستن"
            className="rounded-full p-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
          >
            <X aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="shrink-0 border-b border-border bg-card p-4">
          <Input
            placeholder="جستجوی مدل خودرو (مثال: پراید، تارا...)"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            leftIcon={<Search aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />}
            className="w-full"
          />
        </div>

        <ModalBody className="h-full flex-1 overflow-y-auto bg-card p-4 sm:p-6">
          {isCarsLoading ? (
            <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت لیست خودروها..." />
          ) : filteredCars.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-2 gap-3 py-1 sm:grid-cols-3">
              {filteredCars.map((car: any) => (
                <div
                  key={car.id}
                  onClick={() => {
                    router.push(`/car-parts/${partSlug}/${encodeURIComponent(car.englishTitle)}`);
                    setIsChangeCarOpen(false);
                  }}
                  className={cn(
                    'flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center gap-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-border/80 p-3 text-center transition-all hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-accent/40 active:scale-95',
                    carSlug === car.englishTitle && 'border-primary bg-primary/5 ring-1 ring-primary'
                  )}
                >
                  <div className="relative aspect-[4/3] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-muted/20">
                    <img src={getFullUrl(car.cover)} alt={car.model} className="h-full w-full object-contain p-1" />
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-label truncate w-full">{car.model}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border bg-card py-12 text-center">
              <span className="text-label-muted">خودرویی یافت نشد.</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </ModalBody>
      </Modal>

      <ChangeCategoryModal isOpen={isChangeCatOpen} onClose={() => setIsChangeCatOpen(false)} />
    </div>
  );
}

export default CarPartsContent;

# FILE: src\components\features\Part\components\ChangeCategoryModal.tsx

'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { ListFilter, Search, Settings } from 'lucide-react';
import { useGetPartCategoriesFlat } from '@/domains/front/part/hooks/part.hooks';
import { Modal, ModalHeader, ModalTitle, ModalBody } from '@/components/composites/Modal/Modal';
import { Input } from '@/components/primitives/Input/Input';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { getFullUrl } from '@/core/utils/formatters';

export interface ChangeCategoryModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ChangeCategoryModal({ isOpen, onClose }: ChangeCategoryModalProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = React.useState('');
  const { data: categories = [], isLoading } = useGetPartCategoriesFlat();

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) setSearchQuery('');
  }, [isOpen]);

  const filteredCategories = categories.filter((cat: any) =>
    cat.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  const handleSelectCategory = (cat: any) => {
    // هدایت مستقیم و تمیز به دسته‌بندی جدید
    router.push(`/part-category/${encodeURIComponent(cat.englishTitle)}`);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} className="w-full max-w-lg">
      <ModalHeader onClose={onClose}>
        <ModalTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-title-sm">
          <ListFilter aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
          <span>تغییر دسته‌بندی قطعات</span>
        </ModalTitle>
      </ModalHeader>

      <ModalBody className="flex h-full flex-col gap-4 p-4 pt-2">
        <Input
          type="text"
          placeholder="جستجوی نام دسته‌بندی..."
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          leftIcon={<Search aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />}
          className="w-full"
        />

        {isLoading ? (
          <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت لیست دسته‌بندی‌ها..." />
        ) : filteredCategories.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto pe-1 ps-1 scrollbar-thin md:max-h-80">
            <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-2 gap-3 py-1 sm:grid-cols-3">
              {filteredCategories.map((cat: any) => (
                <Card
                  key={cat.id}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectCategory(cat)}
                  className="flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-3 text-center transition-all hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-accent/30 active:scale-95"
                >
                  <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/40 p-1">
                    {cat.icon || cat.thumbnail ? (
                      <img
                        src={getFullUrl(cat.icon || cat.thumbnail)}
                        alt={cat.name}
                        className="h-full w-full object-contain filter dark:invert"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <Settings aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 text-muted-foreground" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <span className="w-full truncate text-label">{cat.name}</span>
                </Card>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border py-12 text-center">
            <span className="text-label-muted">دسته‌بندی یافت نشد.</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </ModalBody>
    </Modal>
  );
}

# FILE: src\components\features\Part\components\SelectPartModal.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Loader2, Search, SlidersHorizontal, Inbox, Package } from 'lucide-react';
import { useInfiniteQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { getHttpClient } from '@/core/http/client';
import { Modal, ModalHeader, ModalTitle, ModalBody } from '@/components/composites/Modal/Modal';
import { Input } from '@/components/primitives/Input/Input';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { useDebounce } from '@/shared/hooks/useDebounce';
import { getFullUrl } from '@/core/utils/formatters';

export interface SelectPartModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  slug: string; // PartCategoryEnglishTitle یا PartCategoryId
  categoryName: string;
  carSlug?: string;
  categoryId?: string;
}

export function SelectPartModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  slug,
  categoryName,
  carSlug,
  categoryId,
}: SelectPartModalProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [partSearch, setPartSearch] = React.useState('');
  const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(partSearch, 300);

  // تشخیص هوشمند شناسه (GUID) یا عنوان انگلیسی اسلاگ
  const isGuid = /^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}$/.test(slug || '');
  const resolvedCategoryId = categoryId || (isGuid ? slug : undefined);
  const resolvedCategorySlug = !isGuid && slug ? slug : undefined;

  // بازنشانی مقدار اینپوت سرچ با باز شدن مجدد مودال
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setPartSearch('');
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  // ✅ استفاده از useInfiniteQuery پایدار: مشکل خالی شدن در دفعات بعدی را ۱۰۰٪ حل می‌کند
  const {
    data,
    fetchNextPage,
    hasNextPage,
    isFetchingNextPage,
    isLoading,
  } = useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: [
      'front',
      'parts',
      'by-category-infinite',
      resolvedCategoryId,
      resolvedCategorySlug,
      debouncedSearch,
    ],
    queryFn: async ({ pageParam = 1 }) => {
      const client = getHttpClient();
      const params: Record<string, any> = {
        Name: debouncedSearch ? debouncedSearch.trim() : undefined,
        PageNumber: pageParam,
        PageSize: 30,
      };

      if (resolvedCategoryId) {
        params.PartCategoryId = resolvedCategoryId;
      }
      if (resolvedCategorySlug) {
        params.PartCategoryEnglishTitle = resolvedCategorySlug;
      }

      const response = await client.get<any>('/api/Front/PartList', { params });
      return response.data?.pagedParts || response.data || { items: [], totalPages: 1 };
    },
    getNextPageParam: (lastPage, allPages) => {
      const totalPages = lastPage?.totalPages || 1;
      const nextPage = allPages.length + 1;
      return nextPage <= totalPages ? nextPage : undefined;
    },
    initialPageParam: 1,
    enabled: isOpen && !!(resolvedCategoryId || resolvedCategorySlug),
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // ۵ دقیقه کش پایدار در مرورگر
  });

  // تجمیع مستقیم آیتم‌ها از کش بدون وابستگی به useEffect
  const allParts = React.useMemo(() => {
    return data?.pages?.flatMap((page) => page.items || []) || [];
  }, [data]);

  // اسکرول بی‌نهایت برای لود صفحات بعدی
  const handleScroll = (e: React.UIEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (!hasNextPage || isFetchingNextPage || isLoading) return;
    const target = e.currentTarget;
    const isAtBottom = target.scrollHeight - target.scrollTop - target.clientHeight <= 40;
    if (isAtBottom) {
      fetchNextPage();
    }
  };

  const handleSelectPart = (part: any) => {
    let resolvedCarSlug = carSlug;
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const path = window.location.pathname;
      if (path.includes('/car/')) {
        const segments = path.split('/');
        resolvedCarSlug = segments[segments.length - 1];
      }
    }

    if (resolvedCarSlug) {
      router.push(
        `/car-parts/${encodeURIComponent(part.englishTitle || part.id)}/${encodeURIComponent(resolvedCarSlug)}?partId=${part.id}`
      );
      onClose();
      return;
    }

    // هدایت مستقیم و تمیز به صفحه خود قطعه
    const targetCategorySlug =
      resolvedCategorySlug ||
      part.categoryEnglishTitle ||
      resolvedCategoryId ||
      part.categoryId ||
      part.partCategoryId ||
      'category';

    const targetPartSlug = encodeURIComponent(part.englishTitle || part.id);

    router.push(`/parts/${targetCategorySlug}/${targetPartSlug}`);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} className="w-full max-w-lg">
      <ModalHeader onClose={onClose}>
        <ModalTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-title-sm">
          <SlidersHorizontal aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
          <span>انتخاب قطعه برای {categoryName}</span>
        </ModalTitle>
      </ModalHeader>

      <ModalBody className="flex h-full flex-col gap-4 p-4 pt-2">
        <Input
          type="text"
          placeholder="جستجو در نام قطعات..."
          value={partSearch}
          onChange={(e) => setPartSearch(e.target.value)}
          leftIcon={<Search aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />}
          className="w-full"
        />

        <div
          onScroll={handleScroll}
          className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto pe-1 ps-1 scrollbar-thin md:max-h-80"
        >
          {allParts.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-2 gap-3 py-1 sm:grid-cols-3">
              {allParts.map((part: any) => (
                <Card
                  key={part.id}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectPart(part)}
                  className="flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-3 text-center transition-all hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-accent/30 active:scale-95"
                >
                  <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/40 p-1">
                    {part.icon ? (
                      <img
                        src={getFullUrl(part.icon)!}
                        alt={part.name}
                        className="h-full w-full object-contain filter dark:invert"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <Package aria-hidden="true" className="h-6 w-6 text-primary/70" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <span className="w-full truncate text-label">
                    {part.name}
                  </span>
                </Card>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            !isLoading && (
              <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border py-16 text-center">
                <Inbox aria-hidden="true" className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground/60" />
                <span className="text-label-muted">قطعه‌ای یافت نشد.</span>
              </div>
            )
          )}

          {(isFetchingNextPage || (isLoading && allParts.length === 0)) && (
            <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-center py-4">
              <Loader2 aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-primary" />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </ModalBody>
    </Modal>
  );
}

================================================================================
# FILE: src\components\features\ProductCard\ProductSearchCard.tsx
================================================================================
// src/components/features/ProductCard/ProductSearchCard.tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { Star, Store, Package, Plus, Minus, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { useGetBasket, useAddToBasket, useDeleteFromBasket } from '@/domains/front/basket/hooks/basket.hooks';
import { showToast } from '@/core/utils/toast';
import { getProductUrl, toPersianDigits, getFullUrl } from '@/core/utils/formatters';
import { useImpression } from '@/shared/hooks/useImpression';
import { trackShopProductClick } from '@/core/utils/impression-tracker';

export interface ProductSearchCardProps {
  product: any;
  showRating?: boolean;
  showAddToBasket?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

export function ProductSearchCard({
  product,
  showRating = true,
  showAddToBasket = false,
  className,
}: ProductSearchCardProps) {
  const [tickerIndex, setTickerIndex] = React.useState(0);
  const [isMounted, setIsMounted] = React.useState(false);
  const [activeLoading, setActiveLoading] = React.useState(false);

  const { data: rawBasket } = useGetBasket();
  const addToBasket = useAddToBasket();
  const deleteFromBasket = useDeleteFromBasket();

  const rawBasketData = rawBasket as any;
  const lastValidBasketRef = React.useRef<any>(null);

  if (rawBasketData && !rawBasketData.isEmpty) {
    lastValidBasketRef.current = rawBasketData;
  }
  const basket =
    rawBasketData && !rawBasketData.isEmpty ? rawBasketData : lastValidBasketRef.current;

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setIsMounted(true);
  }, []);

  const nominated = product?.nominatedShopProduct || {};
  const shopProductId = product?.shopProductId || nominated?.id || product?.id || null;
  const impressionRef = useImpression(shopProductId);

  const title =
    product?.productTitle || product?.title || product?.name?.value || product?.name || '';

  const shopName = product?.shopTitle || nominated?.shopTitle || product?.shop?.name || '';
  const scoreValue = product?.score ?? nominated?.score ?? product?.averageRate ?? 5;
  const quantityValue = product?.quantity ?? nominated?.quantity ?? 0;

  const originalPriceRaw = Number(
    product?.rialRetailPrice ??
      nominated?.rialRetailPrice ??
      product?.price?.raw ??
      (typeof product?.price === 'number' ? product.price : 0) ??
      nominated?.price ??
      0
  );

  const finalPriceRaw = Number(
    product?.rialFinalPrice ??
      nominated?.rialFinalPrice ??
      (product?.discount?.hasDiscount
        ? product?.price?.raw * (1 - product?.discount?.percent / 100)
        : null) ??
      product?.price?.raw ??
      (typeof product?.price === 'number' ? product.price : 0) ??
      originalPriceRaw
  );

  const originalPriceToman = Math.round(originalPriceRaw / 10);
  const finalPriceToman = Math.round(finalPriceRaw / 10);

  const isOutOfStock =
    finalPriceRaw === 0 || (product?.quantity !== undefined && product.quantity === 0);

  const hasDiscount =
    product?.isDiscountApplied !== undefined
      ? product.isDiscountApplied
      : originalPriceRaw > finalPriceRaw && !isOutOfStock;

  const discountPercent =
    product?.discountPercentage ??
    (hasDiscount && originalPriceRaw > 0
      ? Math.round(((originalPriceRaw - finalPriceRaw) / originalPriceRaw) * 100)
      : nominated?.discountPercentage || product?.discount?.percent || 0);

  const basketItem = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (!basket || basket.isEmpty || !shopProductId) return null;
    for (const sub of basket.subBaskets) {
      const found = sub.items.find((item: any) => item.shopProductId === shopProductId);
      if (found) return found;
    }
    return null;
  }, [basket, shopProductId]);

  const isInBasket = Boolean(basketItem);
  const basketQuantity = basketItem?.quantity || 0;
  const maxLimit =
    basketItem?.maxQuantity ||
    product?.quantity ||
    nominated?.maxQuantityPerOrder ||
    nominated?.quantity ||
    10;

  // ✅ ساخت اسلایدر فاقد موارد تیکر در صورت ناموجود بودن کالا
  const tickerItems = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (isOutOfStock) return [];

    const items: { text: string; icon: any; isShop?: boolean }[] = [];

    const displayShopName = shopName && shopName.trim() !== '' ? shopName : 'یدکچی';
    items.push({
      text: `فروشگاه: ${displayShopName}`,
      icon: Store,
      isShop: true,
    });

    items.push({
      text: `امتیاز: ${toPersianDigits(scoreValue)} از ۵`,
      icon: Star,
    });

    items.push({
      text: quantityValue > 0 ? `موجودی در انبار: ${toPersianDigits(quantityValue)} عدد` : 'وضعیت: ناموجود در انبار',
      icon: Package,
    });

    return items;
  }, [shopName, scoreValue, quantityValue, isOutOfStock]);

  const tickerLength = tickerItems.length;

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!isMounted || tickerLength <= 1 || isOutOfStock) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setTickerIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % tickerLength);
    }, 3000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [tickerLength, isMounted, isOutOfStock]);

  const formatPrice = (value: number) => new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR').format(value);

  const handleAddToBasket = async (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!shopProductId || isOutOfStock) return;
    setActiveLoading(true);
    try {
      await addToBasket.mutateAsync({ shopProductId, quantity: 1 });
      showToast.success('قطعه به سبد خرید اضافه شد');
    } catch (err: any) {
    } finally {
      setActiveLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleIncrease = async (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!shopProductId || activeLoading || basketQuantity >= maxLimit) return;
    setActiveLoading(true);
    try {
      await addToBasket.mutateAsync({ shopProductId, quantity: 1 });
      showToast.success('تعداد کالا افزایش یافت');
    } catch (err: any) {
    } finally {
      setActiveLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDecrease = async (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!shopProductId || activeLoading) return;
    setActiveLoading(true);
    try {
      await deleteFromBasket.mutateAsync({ shopProductId, quantity: 1 });
      showToast.success('تعداد کالا کاهش یافت');
    } catch (err: any) {
    } finally {
      setActiveLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCardClick = () => {
    if (shopProductId) trackShopProductClick(shopProductId);
  };

  const ratingValue = product?.averageRate || product?.rating?.average || scoreValue;
  const ratingCount = product?.rateCount || product?.rating?.count || 0;

  const CurrentTickerIcon = tickerItems[tickerIndex]?.icon || Store;
  const currentItem = tickerItems[tickerIndex];

  const codeOrId =
    product?.productCode || product?.code || product?.productId || product?.id;
  const productCardUrl = getProductUrl(codeOrId, title);

  const imageSource =
    product?.image ||
    product?.images?.[0]?.medium ||
    product?.images?.[0]?.url ||
    product?.images?.[0] ||
    null;

  return (
    <div ref={impressionRef} className={cn('w-full min-w-0 select-none transition-all', className)}>
      
      {/* ================= نسخه دسکتاپ ================= */}
      <Link
        href={productCardUrl}
        prefetch={false}
        onClick={handleCardClick}
        className={cn(
          'relative hidden h-full w-full select-none flex-col rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3 transition-all duration-normal sm:p-3.5 md:flex',
          'hover:border-primary/40 hover:shadow-sm'
        )}
        draggable={false}
      >
        <div className="relative mb-2 aspect-[4/3] w-full select-none overflow-hidden rounded-md" draggable={false}>
          <Image
            src={getFullUrl(imageSource)}
            alt={product?.imageAlt || title}
            fill
            sizes="(max-width: 640px) 50vw, (max-width: 1024px) 33vw, 250px"
            className="select-none rounded-md object-contain"
            draggable={false}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="mb-1 mt-2 h-9 w-full">
          <h4 className="line-clamp-2 text-sm font-semibold leading-relaxed text-foreground">
            {title}
          </h4>
        </div>

        {showRating && !isOutOfStock ? (
          <div className="mt-1 flex w-full select-none items-center justify-start gap-1">
            <Star aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 fill-warning text-warning" />
            <span className="text-2xs font-bold text-foreground sm:text-xs">
              {formatPrice(ratingValue)}
            </span>
            {ratingCount > 0 ? (
              <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
                ({toPersianDigits(ratingCount)})
              </span>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <div className="flex w-full select-none flex-col items-stretch">
          {isMounted && tickerLength > 0 && !isOutOfStock ? (
            <div className="relative mt-1.5 flex h-6 w-full shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-start overflow-hidden text-2xs text-muted-foreground sm:text-xs">
              <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
                <motion.div
                  key={`desktop-ticker-${tickerIndex}`}
                  initial={{ y: 12, opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ y: 0, opacity: 1 }}
                  exit={{ y: -12, opacity: 0 }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: 'easeInOut' }}
                  className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-start gap-1.5 truncate"
                >
                  <CurrentTickerIcon
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    className={cn(
                      'h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0',
                      currentItem?.isShop ? 'text-shop' : 'text-primary'
                    )}
                  />
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      'truncate font-medium',
                      currentItem?.isShop ? 'text-shop font-semibold' : 'text-foreground'
                    )}
                  >
                    {currentItem?.text}
                  </span>
                </motion.div>
              </AnimatePresence>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-1.5 h-6 w-full shrink-0 flex items-center text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
              {isOutOfStock ? 'وضعیت: ناموجود در انبار' : ''}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div
          className={cn(
            'mt-auto flex w-full items-center justify-between pt-2',
            isOutOfStock || !showAddToBasket
              ? 'pb-1'
              : 'border-b border-dashed border-border pb-3.5'
          )}
        >
          <div
            className={cn(
              'shrink-0 rounded-sm border border-accent bg-accent px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-accent-foreground transition-opacity duration-fast',
              hasDiscount && discountPercent > 0 && !isOutOfStock ? 'opacity-100' : 'pointer-events-none opacity-0'
            )}
          >
            {toPersianDigits(discountPercent)}٪
          </div>

          <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col items-end">
            {hasDiscount && originalPriceToman > finalPriceToman && !isOutOfStock ? (
              <span className="text-2xs font-medium text-muted-foreground line-through sm:text-xs">
                {formatPrice(originalPriceToman)}
              </span>
            ) : null}
            <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-0.5">
              {isOutOfStock ? (
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-destructive sm:text-base">ناموجود</span>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <span className="text-base font-bold text-foreground sm:text-lg">
                    {formatPrice(finalPriceToman)}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground">تومان</span>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {showAddToBasket && !isOutOfStock ? (
          <div className="mt-3 h-9 w-full shrink-0">
            {isInBasket ? (
              <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-md border border-primary bg-card p-1 shadow-xs">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleIncrease}
                  disabled={basketQuantity >= maxLimit || activeLoading}
                  aria-label="افزایش تعداد"
                  className="rounded-sm p-1 text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-muted disabled:opacity-30"
                >
                  <Plus aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">
                  {activeLoading ? (
                    <Loader2 aria-hidden="true" className="mx-auto h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-primary" />
                  ) : (
                    new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR').format(basketQuantity)
                  )}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleDecrease}
                  disabled={activeLoading}
                  aria-label="کاهش تعداد"
                  className="rounded-sm p-1 text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
                >
                  <Minus aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                onClick={handleAddToBasket}
                isLoading={activeLoading}
                className="h-full w-full border-primary text-xs text-primary hover:bg-accent"
              >
                <span>افزودن به سبد خرید</span>
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </Link>

      {/* ================= نسخه موبایل ================= */}
      <Link
        href={productCardUrl}
        prefetch={false}
        onClick={handleCardClick}
        className="relative flex w-full min-w-0 select-none flex-col border-b border-border bg-card px-3 py-3.5 md:hidden"
        draggable={false}
      >
        <div className="flex w-full min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
          <div className="relative h-[96px] w-[96px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md bg-muted">
            <Image
              src={getFullUrl(imageSource)}
              alt={product?.imageAlt || title}
              fill
              sizes="96px"
              className="select-none rounded-md object-contain"
              draggable={false}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col justify-between self-stretch">
            <div className="w-full min-w-0">
              <h4 className="line-clamp-2 text-xs font-semibold leading-relaxed text-foreground">
                {title}
              </h4>

              {showRating && !isOutOfStock ? (
                <div className="mt-1 flex select-none items-center justify-start gap-1">
                  <Star aria-hidden="true" className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 fill-warning text-warning" />
                  <span className="text-2xs font-bold text-foreground">
                    {formatPrice(ratingValue)}
                  </span>
                  {ratingCount > 0 ? (
                    <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
                      ({toPersianDigits(ratingCount)})
                    </span>
                  ) : null}
                </div>
              ) : null}

              {isMounted && tickerLength > 0 && !isOutOfStock ? (
                <div className="relative mt-1.5 flex h-5 w-full min-w-0 shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-start overflow-hidden text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
                    <motion.div
                      key={`mobile-ticker-${tickerIndex}`}
                      initial={{ y: 10, opacity: 0 }}
                      animate={{ y: 0, opacity: 1 }}
                      exit={{ y: -10, opacity: 0 }}
                      transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: 'easeInOut' }}
                      className="flex h-full w-full min-w-0 items-center justify-start gap-1.5 truncate"
                    >
                      <CurrentTickerIcon
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className={cn(
                          'h-3 w-3 shrink-0',
                          currentItem?.isShop ? 'text-shop' : 'text-primary'
                        )}
                      />
                      <span
                        className={cn(
                          'truncate text-2xs font-medium',
                          currentItem?.isShop ? 'text-shop font-semibold' : 'text-foreground'
                        )}
                      >
                        {currentItem?.text}
                      </span>
                    </motion.div>
                  </AnimatePresence>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-1.5 h-5 w-full shrink-0 flex items-center text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {isOutOfStock ? 'وضعیت: ناموجود در انبار' : ''}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-2 flex w-full min-w-0 items-end justify-between pt-1">
              <div
                className={cn(
                  'rounded-sm border border-accent bg-accent px-2 py-0.5 text-2xs font-bold text-accent-foreground shrink-0',
                  hasDiscount && discountPercent > 0 && !isOutOfStock
                    ? 'opacity-100'
                    : 'pointer-events-none opacity-0'
                )}
              >
                {toPersianDigits(discountPercent)}٪
              </div>

              <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col items-end">
                {hasDiscount && originalPriceToman > finalPriceToman && !isOutOfStock ? (
                  <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground line-through">
                    {formatPrice(originalPriceToman)}
                  </span>
                ) : null}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                  {isOutOfStock ? (
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-destructive">ناموجود</span>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <span className="text-title-sm">
                        {formatPrice(finalPriceToman)}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground">تومان</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {showAddToBasket && !isOutOfStock ? (
          <div className="mt-3 h-8 w-full shrink-0">
            {isInBasket ? (
              <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-md border border-primary bg-card p-1 shadow-xs">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleIncrease}
                  disabled={basketQuantity >= maxLimit || activeLoading}
                  aria-label="افزایش تعداد"
                  className="rounded-sm p-1 text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
                >
                  <Plus aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </button>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">
                  {activeLoading ? (
                    <Loader2 aria-hidden="true" className="mx-auto h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin text-primary" />
                  ) : (
                    new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR').format(basketQuantity)
                  )}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleDecrease}
                  disabled={activeLoading}
                  aria-label="کاهش تعداد"
                  className="rounded-sm p-1 text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
                >
                  <Minus aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                onClick={handleAddToBasket}
                isLoading={activeLoading}
                className="h-full w-full border-primary text-xs text-primary hover:bg-accent"
              >
                <span>افزودن به سبد خرید</span>
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
# FILE: src\components\features\ProductCard\ShopProductAdBanner.tsx
================================================================================
// src/components/features/ProductCard/ShopProductAdBanner.tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { Star, Store, Sparkles, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { useGetShopProductBanners } from '@/domains/front/banner/hooks/banner.hooks';
import { useBannerImpression } from '@/shared/hooks/useBannerImpression';
import { bannerTracker } from '@/core/utils/banner-tracker';
import { toPersianDigits, getProductUrl, getFullUrl } from '@/core/utils/formatters';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

export interface ShopProductAdBannerProps {
  partCategoryId?: string;
  partId?: string;
  carId?: string;
  className?: string;
}

export function ShopProductAdBanner({
  partCategoryId,
  partId,
  carId,
  className,
}: ShopProductAdBannerProps) {
  const { data: banners = [], isLoading } = useGetShopProductBanners({
    partCategoryId,
    partId,
    carId,
  });

  const ad = banners[0];
  const impressionRef = useBannerImpression(ad?.id || null, ad?.product?.id || null);

  if (isLoading || !ad) return null;
  const adProductUrl = getProductUrl(ad.product.code, ad.product.name);

  const shopName = ad.product.shopName || 'یدکچی';
  const hasDiscount = ad.product.discount.hasDiscount && !!ad.product.discount.discountPrice;
  const discountPercent = ad.product.discount.discountPercent || 0;

  return (
    <div className={cn('relative w-full select-none', className)} ref={impressionRef}>
      
      {/* ================= نسخه دسکتاپ (کارت عمودی لوکس و پرمخاطب) ================= */}
      <Link
        href={adProductUrl}
        className="hidden h-full w-full select-none md:block group/ad"
        onClick={() => bannerTracker.trackClick(ad.id, ad.product.id)}
      >
        <div
          className={cn(
            'relative flex w-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-gradient-to-b from-card via-card to-muted/30 p-4 shadow-xs transition-all duration-300',
            'hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-primary/50 hover:shadow-md'
          )}
        >
          {/* هدر کارت: تگ منتخب */}
          <div className="absolute end-3 top-3 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-primary/10 border border-primary/20 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-2xs font-bold text-primary backdrop-blur-md">
            <Sparkles className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
            <span>منتخب یدکچی</span>
          </div>

          {/* تصویر محصول */}
          <div className="relative mb-3 flex aspect-[4/3] w-full items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-2">
            <Image
              src={getFullUrl(ad.imageUrl)}
              alt={ad.title}
              fill
              sizes="250px"
              className="object-contain transition-transform duration-500 ease-out group-hover/ad:scale-105"
            />
          </div>

          {/* اطلاعات کالا */}
          <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 min-h-[4.5rem]">
            <h4 className="line-clamp-2 text-xs font-bold leading-relaxed text-foreground group-hover/ad:text-primary transition-colors">
              {ad.product.name}
            </h4>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0">
                <Store aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground/80" />
                <span className="truncate">فروشنده: {shopName}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                <Star aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 fill-warning text-warning" />
                <span className="font-bold text-foreground">۵.۰</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* فوتر کارت: قیمت و تخفیف */}
          <div className="mt-3 flex w-full items-center justify-between border-t border-border/70 pt-3">
            {hasDiscount && discountPercent > 0 ? (
              <div className="rounded-full bg-destructive/10 border border-destructive/20 px-2 py-0.5 text-2xs font-bold text-destructive">
                {toPersianDigits(discountPercent)}٪ تخفیف
              </div>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground font-medium">ارسال فوری</span>
            )}

            <div className="flex flex-col items-end">
              {hasDiscount ? (
                <span className="text-2xs leading-none text-muted-foreground line-through">
                  {ad.product.price.toman}
                </span>
              ) : null}
              <span className="mt-1 text-xs font-extrabold leading-none text-foreground">
                {hasDiscount ? ad.product.discount.discountPrice : ad.product.price.toman}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Link>

      {/* ================= نسخه موبایل (بنر افقی مینی‌مال و مدرن) ================= */}
      <Link
        href={adProductUrl}
        className="block w-full select-none md:hidden group/ad-mobile"
        onClick={() => bannerTracker.trackClick(ad.id, ad.product.id)}
      >
        <div
          className={cn(
            'relative flex h-24 w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-gradient-to-r from-card via-card to-muted/30 p-3 shadow-xs transition-all duration-300',
            'active:scale-[0.99] hover:border-primary/40 hover:shadow-sm'
          )}
        >
          {/* عکس محصول در موبایل */}
          <div className="relative h-20 w-20 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-1.5">
            <Image
              src={getFullUrl(ad.imageUrl)}
              alt={ad.title}
              fill
              sizes="80px"
              className="object-contain transition-transform duration-300 group-hover/ad-mobile:scale-105"
            />
          </div>

          {/* متن و فروشنده */}
          <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col justify-between self-stretch py-0.5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full bg-primary/10 px-2 py-0.2 text-3xs font-bold text-primary">
                <Sparkles className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
                منتخب
              </span>
              <span className="text-3xs text-muted-foreground truncate">فروشگاه: {shopName}</span>
            </div>

            <h4 className="line-clamp-2 text-xs font-bold leading-tight text-foreground group-hover/ad-mobile:text-primary transition-colors">
              {ad.product.name}
            </h4>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
              <Star aria-hidden="true" className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 fill-warning text-warning" />
              <span className="font-bold text-foreground">۵.۰</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* قیمت و اکشن */}
          <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-col items-end justify-between self-stretch py-0.5 ps-2">
            {hasDiscount && discountPercent > 0 ? (
              <span className="rounded-full bg-destructive/10 px-2 py-0.2 text-3xs font-bold text-destructive">
                {toPersianDigits(discountPercent)}٪
              </span>
            ) : (
              <div />
            )}

            <div className="flex flex-col items-end">
              {hasDiscount ? (
                <span className="text-3xs leading-none text-muted-foreground line-through">
                  {ad.product.price.toman}
                </span>
              ) : null}
              <span className="mt-1 text-xs font-black leading-none text-foreground">
                {hasDiscount ? ad.product.discount.discountPrice : ad.product.price.toman}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
# FILE: src\components\features\ProductGrid\SearchSidebar.tsx
