'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';

import { useGetBrandsName } from '@/domains/front/reference/brand/hooks/brand.hooks';
import { useGetCarsName } from '@/domains/front/reference/car/hooks/car.hooks';
import {
  useGetPartsName,
  useGetPartCategories,
} from '@/domains/front/part/hooks/part.hooks';

import { Accordion } from '@/components/composites/Accordion/Accordion';
import { Switch } from '@/components/primitives/Switch/Switch';
import { Checkbox } from '@/components/primitives/Checkbox/Checkbox';
import { PriceSlider } from '@/components/composites/PriceSlider/PriceSlider';
import {
  FilterList,
  FilterItem,
} from '@/components/composites/FilterList/FilterList';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { Typography } from '@/components/primitives/Typography';
import { X, Filter, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

import { SearchProductsRequest } from '@/domains/front/product/types/view.types';
import { Modal } from '@/components/composites/Modal/Modal';
import { ShopProductAdBanner } from '@/components/features/ProductCard/ShopProductAdBanner';
import { toPersianDigits } from '@/core/utils/formatters';

// ============================================================
// Types
// ============================================================

interface AggregationBucket {
  key: string;
  count: number;
}

interface SearchAggregations {
  brands?: AggregationBucket[];
  parts?: AggregationBucket[];
  partCategories?: AggregationBucket[];
  carIds?: AggregationBucket[];
  cars?: AggregationBucket[];
  types?: AggregationBucket[];

  priceStats?: {
    min: number;
    max: number;
    avg?: number;
    sum?: number;
  };
}

interface AggregationMemoryBank {
  brands: Map<string, number>;
  parts: Map<string, number>;
  categories: Map<string, number>;
  cars: Map<string, number>;
  types: Map<string, number>;
}

interface PersistedAggregationMemory {
  version: 1;
  savedAt: number;
  data: {
    brands: [string, number][];
    parts: [string, number][];
    categories: [string, number][];
    cars: [string, number][];
    types: [string, number][];
  };
}

interface AggregationMemoryState {
  scopeKey: string;
  bank: AggregationMemoryBank;
}

interface SearchSidebarProps {
  filters: SearchProductsRequest;
  aggregations?: SearchAggregations | null;

  onFilterChange: (name: string, value: any) => void;
  onClearAll: () => void;

  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;

  hidePartFilter?: boolean;

  carId?: string;
  partCategoryId?: string;
  partId?: string;
}

// ============================================================
// Configuration
// ============================================================

const MEMORY_STORAGE_PREFIX = 'product-search-aggregation:v1:';

// اعتبار Cache: 24 ساعت
const MEMORY_TTL = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

// ============================================================
// Memory Utilities
// ============================================================

function createEmptyMemoryBank(): AggregationMemoryBank {
  return {
    brands: new Map(),
    parts: new Map(),
    categories: new Map(),
    cars: new Map(),
    types: new Map(),
  };
}

function normalizeKey(value: unknown): string {
  return String(value).toLowerCase();
}

function getMemoryStorageKey(scopeKey: string): string {
  return `${MEMORY_STORAGE_PREFIX}${encodeURIComponent(scopeKey)}`;
}

/**
 * خواندن حافظه از localStorage
 *
 * در SSR یا در صورت خراب بودن اطلاعات ذخیره‌شده،
 * یک حافظه خالی برمی‌گرداند.
 */
function readAggregationMemory(
  scopeKey: string
): AggregationMemoryBank {
  const empty = createEmptyMemoryBank();

  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return empty;
  }

  try {
    const storageKey = getMemoryStorageKey(scopeKey);
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);

    if (!raw) {
      return empty;
    }

    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as PersistedAggregationMemory;

    // بررسی نسخه ساختار
    if (parsed.version !== 1 || !parsed.data) {
      window.localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
      return empty;
    }

    // بررسی انقضای Cache
    if (
      !parsed.savedAt ||
      Date.now() - parsed.savedAt > MEMORY_TTL
    ) {
      window.localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
      return empty;
    }

    const restoreMap = (
      entries: unknown
    ): Map<string, number> => {
      if (!Array.isArray(entries)) {
        return new Map();
      }

      const result = new Map<string, number>();

      for (const entry of entries) {
        if (
          !Array.isArray(entry) ||
          entry.length !== 2 ||
          typeof entry[0] !== 'string' ||
          typeof entry[1] !== 'number' ||
          !Number.isFinite(entry[1])
        ) {
          continue;
        }

        result.set(normalizeKey(entry[0]), entry[1]);
      }

      return result;
    };

    return {
      brands: restoreMap(parsed.data.brands),
      parts: restoreMap(parsed.data.parts),
      categories: restoreMap(parsed.data.categories),
      cars: restoreMap(parsed.data.cars),
      types: restoreMap(parsed.data.types),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      '[SearchSidebar] Failed to restore aggregation memory:',
      error
    );

    return empty;
  }
}

/**
 * ذخیره حافظه در localStorage
 */
function persistAggregationMemory(
  scopeKey: string,
  bank: AggregationMemoryBank
): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return;
  }

  try {
    const payload: PersistedAggregationMemory = {
      version: 1,
      savedAt: Date.now(),

      data: {
        brands: Array.from(bank.brands.entries()),
        parts: Array.from(bank.parts.entries()),
        categories: Array.from(bank.categories.entries()),
        cars: Array.from(bank.cars.entries()),
        types: Array.from(bank.types.entries()),
      },
    };

    window.localStorage.setItem(
      getMemoryStorageKey(scopeKey),
      JSON.stringify(payload)
    );
  } catch (error) {
    // ممکن است Storage پر باشد یا دسترسی مرورگر محدود شده باشد.
    console.error(
      '[SearchSidebar] Failed to persist aggregation memory:',
      error
    );
  }
}

/**
 * ترکیب Aggregation جدید سرور با حافظه قبلی
 *
 * اطلاعات جدید جایگزین مقادیر قبلی همان کلید می‌شوند.
 * کلیدهایی که در پاسخ جدید وجود ندارند حفظ می‌شوند.
 */
function mergeAggregations(
  previous: AggregationMemoryBank,
  aggregations: SearchAggregations
): AggregationMemoryBank {
  const next: AggregationMemoryBank = {
    brands: new Map(previous.brands),
    parts: new Map(previous.parts),
    categories: new Map(previous.categories),
    cars: new Map(previous.cars),
    types: new Map(previous.types),
  };

  (aggregations.brands || []).forEach((item) => {
    next.brands.set(normalizeKey(item.key), item.count);
  });

  (aggregations.parts || []).forEach((item) => {
    next.parts.set(normalizeKey(item.key), item.count);
  });

  (aggregations.partCategories || []).forEach((item) => {
    next.categories.set(normalizeKey(item.key), item.count);
  });

  const carSource = aggregations.carIds || aggregations.cars || [];

  carSource.forEach((item) => {
    next.cars.set(normalizeKey(item.key), item.count);
  });

  (aggregations.types || []).forEach((item) => {
    const key = normalizeKey(item.key);

    if (key === '1' || key === 'new') {
      next.types.set('new', item.count);
    } else if (key === '2' || key === 'stock') {
      next.types.set('stock', item.count);
    } else if (key === '3' || key === 'takeoff') {
      next.types.set('takeoff', item.count);
    }
  });

  return next;
}

// ============================================================
// Component
// ============================================================

export function SearchSidebar({
  filters,
  aggregations,
  onFilterChange,
  onClearAll,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  hidePartFilter = false,
  carId,
  partCategoryId,
  partId,
}: SearchSidebarProps) {
  // ==========================================================
  // Search states
  // ==========================================================

  const [brandSearch, setBrandSearch] = useState('');
  const [carSearch, setCarSearch] = useState('');
  const [partSearch, setPartSearch] = useState('');
  const [categorySearch, setCategorySearch] = useState('');

  // ==========================================================
  // Aggregation scope
  // ==========================================================

  /**
   * هر جستجو حافظه مستقل دارد.
   *
   * تغییر Search باعث پاک شدن Cache قبلی نمی‌شود.
   * در صورت بازگشت به همان جستجو، Cache قبلی بازیابی می‌شود.
   */
  const aggregationScopeKey = useMemo(() => {
    return JSON.stringify({
      searchTitle: filters.searchTitle || '',
      partCategoryEnglishTitle:
        filters.partCategoryEnglishTitle || '',
      carId: carId || '',
      partCategoryId: partCategoryId || '',
      partId: partId || '',
    });
  }, [
    filters.searchTitle,
    filters.partCategoryEnglishTitle,
    carId,
    partCategoryId,
    partId,
  ]);

  // ==========================================================
  // API queries
  // ==========================================================

  const relevantCategoryId =
    filters.partCategoryIds?.[0] ||
    (
      aggregations?.partCategories &&
      aggregations.partCategories.length > 0
        ? aggregations.partCategories[0].key
        : undefined
    );

  const { data: brandsList = [] } = useGetBrandsName({});

  const { data: carsList = [] } = useGetCarsName({});

  const { data: categoriesList = [] } = useGetPartCategories();

  const { data: partsData } = useGetPartsName({
    name: partSearch || filters.searchTitle || undefined,
    partCategoryId: relevantCategoryId,
    pageNumber: 1,
    pageSize: 100,
  });

  // ==========================================================
  // Persistent Aggregation Memory
  // ==========================================================

  /**
   * State به scope خودش وابسته است.
   *
   * این ساختار مانع می‌شود اطلاعات یک جستجو
   * اشتباهاً در جستجوی دیگر نمایش داده شوند.
   *
   * مقدار اولیه خالی است تا SSR و Hydration
   * با localStorage اختلاف نداشته باشند.
   */

  const [memoryState, setMemoryState] =
    useState<AggregationMemoryState>(() => ({
      scopeKey: '',
      bank: createEmptyMemoryBank(),
    }));

  /**
   * بازیابی حافظه هنگام ورود یا تغییر محدوده جستجو
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    const restoredMemory =
      readAggregationMemory(aggregationScopeKey);

    setMemoryState({
      scopeKey: aggregationScopeKey,
      bank: restoredMemory,
    });
  }, [aggregationScopeKey]);

  /**
   * ذخیره تغییرات حافظه
   *
   * فقط زمانی ذخیره می‌کنیم که حافظه متعلق به
   * محدوده جستجوی فعلی باشد.
   *
   * این شرط از ذخیره اطلاعات جستجوی قبلی
   * تحت کلید جستجوی جدید جلوگیری می‌کند.
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (memoryState.scopeKey !== aggregationScopeKey) {
      return;
    }

    persistAggregationMemory(
      aggregationScopeKey,
      memoryState.bank
    );
  }, [memoryState, aggregationScopeKey]);

  /**
   * دریافت و ترکیب Aggregation جدید
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!aggregations) {
      return;
    }

    setMemoryState((previous) => {
      // جلوگیری از ورود اطلاعات به Scope اشتباه
      if (previous.scopeKey !== aggregationScopeKey) {
        return previous;
      }

      return {
        scopeKey: aggregationScopeKey,
        bank: mergeAggregations(
          previous.bank,
          aggregations
        ),
      };
    });
  }, [aggregations, aggregationScopeKey]);

  /**
   * حافظه قابل استفاده برای رندر
   */
  const memoryBank =
    memoryState.scopeKey === aggregationScopeKey
      ? memoryState.bank
      : createEmptyMemoryBank();

  const brandAggMap = memoryBank.brands;
  const partAggMap = memoryBank.parts;
  const categoryAggMap = memoryBank.categories;
  const carAggMap = memoryBank.cars;
  const typeAggMap = memoryBank.types;

  // ==========================================================
  // Categories
  // ==========================================================

  const formattedCategories: FilterItem[] = useMemo(() => {
    const selectedIds = new Set(
      (filters.partCategoryIds || []).map(normalizeKey)
    );

    const items = categoriesList
      .filter((category: any) => {
        const id = normalizeKey(category.id);

        return (
          categoryAggMap.has(id) ||
          selectedIds.has(id)
        );
      })
      .map((category: any) => ({
        id: category.id,
        name: category.name,
        count:
          categoryAggMap.get(normalizeKey(category.id)) || 0,
      }));

    if (categorySearch.trim()) {
      return items.filter((category: any) =>
        category.name
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(categorySearch.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    return items;
  }, [
    categoriesList,
    categoryAggMap,
    filters.partCategoryIds,
    categorySearch,
  ]);

  // ==========================================================
  // Brands
  // ==========================================================

  const formattedBrands: FilterItem[] = useMemo(() => {
    const selectedIds = new Set(
      (filters.brandIds || []).map(normalizeKey)
    );

    const items = brandsList
      .filter((brand: any) => {
        const id = normalizeKey(brand.id);

        return (
          brandAggMap.has(id) ||
          selectedIds.has(id)
        );
      })
      .map((brand: any) => ({
        id: brand.id,
        name: brand.name,
        count:
          brandAggMap.get(normalizeKey(brand.id)) || 0,
      }));

    if (brandSearch.trim()) {
      return items.filter((brand: any) =>
        brand.name
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(brandSearch.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    return items;
  }, [
    brandsList,
    brandAggMap,
    filters.brandIds,
    brandSearch,
  ]);

  // ==========================================================
  // Parts
  // ==========================================================

  const allParts = partsData?.items || [];

  const formattedParts: FilterItem[] = useMemo(() => {
    const selectedIds = new Set(
      (filters.partIds || []).map(normalizeKey)
    );

    let items = allParts
      .filter((part: any) => {
        const id = normalizeKey(part.id);

        return (
          partAggMap.has(id) ||
          selectedIds.has(id)
        );
      })
      .map((part: any) => ({
        id: part.id,
        name: part.name,
        count:
          partAggMap.get(normalizeKey(part.id)) || 0,
      }));

    /**
     * در صورت نبودن اطلاعات نام قطعات در API،
     * شناسه‌های موجود در Aggregation حفظ می‌شوند.
     */
    if (items.length === 0 && partAggMap.size > 0) {
      items = Array.from(partAggMap.entries()).map(
        ([key, count]) => ({
          id: key,
          name: filters.searchTitle
            ? `قطعات مرتبط با «${filters.searchTitle}»`
            : 'قطعه مرتبط',
          count,
        })
      );
    }

    if (partSearch.trim()) {
      return items.filter((part: any) =>
        part.name
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(partSearch.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    return items;
  }, [
    allParts,
    partAggMap,
    filters.partIds,
    partSearch,
    filters.searchTitle,
  ]);

  // ==========================================================
  // Cars
  // ==========================================================

  const formattedCars: FilterItem[] = useMemo(() => {
    const selectedIds = new Set(
      (filters.carIds || []).map(normalizeKey)
    );

    const items = carsList
      .filter((car: any) => {
        const id = normalizeKey(car.id);

        return (
          carAggMap.has(id) ||
          selectedIds.has(id)
        );
      })
      .map((car: any) => ({
        id: car.id,
        name: car.displayName || car.model,
        count:
          carAggMap.get(normalizeKey(car.id)) || 0,
      }));

    if (carSearch.trim()) {
      return items.filter((car: any) =>
        car.name
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(carSearch.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    return items;
  }, [
    carsList,
    carAggMap,
    filters.carIds,
    carSearch,
  ]);

  // ==========================================================
  // Price
  // ==========================================================

  const dynamicMinPrice =
    aggregations?.priceStats?.min ?? 50000;

  const dynamicMaxPrice =
    aggregations?.priceStats?.max ?? 100000000;

  const [priceRange, setPriceRange] = useState<
    [number, number]
  >([
    filters.fromPrice || dynamicMinPrice,
    filters.toPrice || dynamicMaxPrice,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setPriceRange([
      filters.fromPrice || dynamicMinPrice,
      filters.toPrice || dynamicMaxPrice,
    ]);
  }, [
    filters.fromPrice,
    filters.toPrice,
    dynamicMinPrice,
    dynamicMaxPrice,
  ]);

  // ==========================================================
  // Filter handlers
  // ==========================================================

  const handleCheckboxChange = (
    name: string,
    id: string,
    checked: boolean
  ) => {
    const currentList =
      (filters[name as keyof SearchProductsRequest] as
        | string[]
        | undefined) || [];

    let updatedList = [...currentList];

    if (checked) {
      if (!updatedList.some((item) => normalizeKey(item) === normalizeKey(id))) {
        updatedList.push(id);
      }
    } else {
      updatedList = updatedList.filter(
        (item) => normalizeKey(item) !== normalizeKey(id)
      );
    }

    onFilterChange(
      name,
      updatedList.length > 0 ? updatedList : undefined
    );
  };

  const handleTypeChange = (
    type: 'New' | 'Stock' | 'TakeOff',
    checked: boolean
  ) => {
    const currentTypes =
      (filters.types as string[]) || [];

    let updatedTypes = [...currentTypes];

    if (checked) {
      if (!updatedTypes.includes(type)) {
        updatedTypes.push(type);
      }
    } else {
      updatedTypes = updatedTypes.filter(
        (item) => item !== type
      );
    }

    onFilterChange(
      'types',
      updatedTypes.length > 0 ? updatedTypes : undefined
    );
  };

  // ==========================================================
  // Active filters
  // ==========================================================

  const hasActiveFilters = useMemo(() => {
    return !!(
      filters.onlyInStock ||
      filters.onlyDiscounted ||
      filters.onlyDirectShipping ||
      filters.onlyNominated ||
      filters.fromPrice ||
      filters.toPrice ||
      filters.brandIds?.length ||
      filters.carIds?.length ||
      filters.partCategoryIds?.length ||
      filters.partIds?.length ||
      filters.types?.length
    );
  }, [filters]);

  const activeTypesCount = filters.types?.length || 0;
  const activeCategoriesCount =
    filters.partCategoryIds?.length || 0;
  const activePartsCount = filters.partIds?.length || 0;
  const activeBrandsCount = filters.brandIds?.length || 0;
  const activeCarsCount = filters.carIds?.length || 0;

  // فقط فیلتر انتخابی قیمت، فعال بودن فیلتر را مشخص می‌کند.
  const isPriceFilterActive = !!(
    filters.fromPrice || filters.toPrice
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // Type availability
  // ==========================================================

  const selectedTypes = (filters.types as string[]) || [];

  const isNewAvailable =
    (typeAggMap.get('new') || 0) > 0 ||
    selectedTypes.includes('New');

  const isStockAvailable =
    (typeAggMap.get('stock') || 0) > 0 ||
    selectedTypes.includes('Stock');

  const isTakeOffAvailable =
    (typeAggMap.get('takeoff') || 0) > 0 ||
    selectedTypes.includes('TakeOff');

  // ==========================================================
  // Helpers
  // ==========================================================

  const formatCount = (count: number) => {
    return count > 0
      ? ` (${toPersianDigits(count)})`
      : '';
  };

  // ==========================================================
  // Filter content
  // ==========================================================

  const renderFilterContent = (isMobile: boolean) => (
    <div
      className={cn(
        'flex w-full flex-col gap-4',
        isMobile && 'pb-24'
      )}
      dir="rtl"
    >
      {/* General filters */}

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3.5 border-b border-border/80 pb-4">
        <div className="flex w-full select-none items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">
            فقط کالاهای موجود
          </span>

          <Switch
            checked={!!filters.onlyInStock}
            onChange={(checked) =>
              onFilterChange(
                'inStock',
                checked ? true : undefined
              )
            }
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex w-full select-none items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">
            دارای تخفیف ویژه
          </span>

          <Switch
            checked={!!filters.onlyDiscounted}
            onChange={(checked) =>
              onFilterChange(
                'discount',
                checked ? true : undefined
              )
            }
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex w-full select-none items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">
            ارسال مستقیم از فروشنده
          </span>

          <Switch
            checked={!!filters.onlyDirectShipping}
            onChange={(checked) =>
              onFilterChange(
                'directShipping',
                checked ? true : undefined
              )
            }
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex w-full select-none items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">
            فقط کالاهای برگزیده
          </span>

          <Switch
            checked={!!filters.onlyNominated}
            onChange={(checked) =>
              onFilterChange(
                'nominated',
                checked ? true : undefined
              )
            }
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Part types */}

      {(isNewAvailable ||
        isStockAvailable ||
        isTakeOffAvailable) && (
        <Accordion
          title={`نوع قطعه${formatCount(activeTypesCount)}`}
          defaultOpen={activeTypesCount > 0}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 pe-1 pt-1 text-xs">
            <Checkbox
              label="همه قطعات"
              checked={
                !filters.types ||
                filters.types.length === 0
              }
              onChange={(checked) =>
                checked &&
                onFilterChange('types', undefined)
              }
            />

            {isNewAvailable && (
              <Checkbox
                label={`نو ${
                  typeAggMap.get('new')
                    ? `(${toPersianDigits(
                        typeAggMap.get('new')!
                      )})`
                    : ''
                }`}
                checked={selectedTypes.includes('New')}
                onChange={(checked) =>
                  handleTypeChange('New', checked)
                }
              />
            )}

            {isStockAvailable && (
              <Checkbox
                label={`استوک ${
                  typeAggMap.get('stock')
                    ? `(${toPersianDigits(
                        typeAggMap.get('stock')!
                      )})`
                    : ''
                }`}
                checked={selectedTypes.includes('Stock')}
                onChange={(checked) =>
                  handleTypeChange('Stock', checked)
                }
              />
            )}

            {isTakeOffAvailable && (
              <Checkbox
                label={`زیرصفری ${
                  typeAggMap.get('takeoff')
                    ? `(${toPersianDigits(
                        typeAggMap.get('takeoff')!
                      )})`
                    : ''
                }`}
                checked={selectedTypes.includes('TakeOff')}
                onChange={(checked) =>
                  handleTypeChange('TakeOff', checked)
                }
              />
            )}
          </div>
        </Accordion>
      )}

      {/* Categories */}

      {formattedCategories.length > 0 && (
        <Accordion
          title={`دسته‌بندی قطعه${formatCount(
            activeCategoriesCount
          )}`}
          defaultOpen={activeCategoriesCount > 0}
        >
          <FilterList
            items={formattedCategories}
            selectedIds={
              (filters.partCategoryIds as string[]) || []
            }
            onChange={(id, checked) =>
              handleCheckboxChange(
                'partCategoryIds',
                String(id),
                checked
              )
            }
            searchPlaceholder="جستجوی دسته‌بندی..."
            onSearchChange={setCategorySearch}
          />
        </Accordion>
      )}

      {/* Parts */}

      {!hidePartFilter && formattedParts.length > 0 && (
        <Accordion
          title={`قطعه${formatCount(activePartsCount)}`}
          defaultOpen={activePartsCount > 0}
        >
          <FilterList
            items={formattedParts}
            selectedIds={
              (filters.partIds as string[]) || []
            }
            onChange={(id, checked) =>
              handleCheckboxChange(
                'partIds',
                String(id),
                checked
              )
            }
            searchPlaceholder="جستجوی نام قطعه..."
            onSearchChange={setPartSearch}
          />
        </Accordion>
      )}

      {/* Brands */}

      {formattedBrands.length > 0 && (
        <Accordion
          title={`برند قطعه${formatCount(activeBrandsCount)}`}
          defaultOpen={activeBrandsCount > 0}
        >
          <FilterList
            items={formattedBrands}
            selectedIds={
              (filters.brandIds as string[]) || []
            }
            onChange={(id, checked) =>
              handleCheckboxChange(
                'brandIds',
                String(id),
                checked
              )
            }
            searchPlaceholder="جستجوی برند..."
            onSearchChange={setBrandSearch}
          />
        </Accordion>
      )}

      {/* Cars */}

      {formattedCars.length > 0 && (
        <Accordion
          title={`مدل خودرو${formatCount(activeCarsCount)}`}
          defaultOpen={activeCarsCount > 0}
        >
          <FilterList
            items={formattedCars}
            selectedIds={
              (filters.carIds as string[]) || []
            }
            onChange={(id, checked) =>
              handleCheckboxChange(
                'carIds',
                String(id),
                checked
              )
            }
            searchPlaceholder="جستجوی مدل خودرو..."
            onSearchChange={setCarSearch}
          />
        </Accordion>
      )}

      {/* Price */}

      <Accordion
        title={
          isPriceFilterActive
            ? 'محدوده قیمت (فعال)'
            : 'محدوده قیمت'
        }
        defaultOpen={isPriceFilterActive}
      >
        <PriceSlider
          min={dynamicMinPrice}
          max={dynamicMaxPrice}
          value={priceRange}
          onChange={(value) => setPriceRange(value)}
          onApply={() => {
            onFilterChange('fromPrice', priceRange[0]);
            onFilterChange('toPrice', priceRange[1]);
          }}
        />
      </Accordion>
    </div>
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // Render
  // ==========================================================

  return (
    <>
      {/* Desktop Sidebar */}

      <aside
        style={{
          top: 'calc(var(--header-height, 108px) + 0.75rem)',
          maxHeight:
            'calc(100vh - var(--header-height, 108px) - 1.5rem)',
        }}
        className="hidden w-[18rem] shrink-0 sticky flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto scrollbar-thin select-none lg:flex"
        dir="rtl"
      >
        <ShopProductAdBanner
          carId={carId}
          partCategoryId={partCategoryId}
          partId={partId}
        />

        <div className="h-fit w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-4 shadow-xs">
          <div className="mb-4 flex w-full select-none items-center justify-between border-b border-border pb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Filter className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />

              <Typography
                variant="h5"
                className="font-bold text-foreground"
              >
                فیلترها
              </Typography>
            </div>

            {hasActiveFilters && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClearAll}
                className="text-xs font-bold text-destructive transition-colors hover:underline"
              >
                حذف همه
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {renderFilterContent(false)}
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Mobile Sidebar */}

      <Modal
        isOpen={isOpen}
        onClose={onClose}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-modal flex h-full max-h-full w-full max-w-none flex-col rounded-none bg-card p-0"
        overlayClassName="bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm"
      >
        {/* Header */}

        <div
          className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b border-border bg-muted/20 px-4 py-3.5"
          dir="rtl"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-full p-1 text-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
              aria-label="بستن پنجره"
            >
              <ArrowRight className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>

            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold sm:text-sm">
              <Filter className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
              فیلترها
            </span>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full p-1.5 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground"
            aria-label="بستن پنجره"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content */}

        <div
          className="relative flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 scrollbar-thin"
          dir="rtl"
        >
          {renderFilterContent(true)}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}

        <div
          className="sticky bottom-0 right-0 left-0 z-sticky flex shrink-0 gap-3 border-t border-border bg-card p-4 shadow-lg"
          dir="rtl"
        >
          {hasActiveFilters && (
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => {
                onClearAll();
                onClose();
              }}
              className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-xl border-destructive/20 text-xs font-bold text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/5"
            >
              حذف فیلترها
            </Button>
          )}

          <Button
            variant="primary"
            size="sm"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
          >
            مشاهده نتایج
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
}

# FILE: src\components\primitives\Button\Button.tsx
================================================================================
import * as React from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';
import { buttonVariants, type ButtonVariantProps } from '@/design-system/variants/button.variants';

const DotsLoader = () => (
  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1" aria-hidden="true">
    <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-bounce [animation-delay:-0.3s]" />
    <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-bounce [animation-delay:-0.15s]" />
    <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-bounce" />
  </span>
);

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,
    ButtonVariantProps {
  isLoading?: boolean;
  loadingText?: string;
  leftIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  rightIcon?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  (
    {
      className,
      variant,
      size,
      fullWidth,
      isLoading = false,
      loadingText,
      children,
      disabled,
      leftIcon,
      rightIcon,
      type = 'button',
      ...props
    },
    ref
  ) => {
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        type={type}
        disabled={disabled || isLoading}
        aria-busy={isLoading || undefined}
        className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, fullWidth }), className)}
        {...props}
      >
        {!isLoading && leftIcon ? <span aria-hidden="true">{leftIcon}</span> : null}
        {isLoading ? (
          loadingText ? (
            <>
              <span>{loadingText}</span>
              <DotsLoader />
            </>
          ) : (
            <DotsLoader />
          )
        ) : (
          children
        )}
        {!isLoading && rightIcon ? <span aria-hidden="true">{rightIcon}</span> : null}
      </button>
    );
  }
);

Button.displayName = 'Button';

# FILE: src\components\primitives\Checkbox\Checkbox.tsx
================================================================================
import * as React from 'react';
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

export interface CheckboxProps {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
  label?: string;
  className?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  id?: string;
}

export const Checkbox = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, CheckboxProps>(
  ({ checked, onChange, label, className, disabled, id }, ref) => {
    const generatedId = React.useId();
    const checkboxId = id ?? generatedId;

    return (
      <label
        htmlFor={checkboxId}
        className={cn(
          'group flex items-center gap-2.5 select-none',
          disabled ? 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-50' : 'cursor-pointer',
          className
        )}
      >
        <button
          ref={ref}
          id={checkboxId}
          type="button"
          role="checkbox"
          aria-checked={checked}
          disabled={disabled}
          onClick={() => !disabled && onChange(!checked)}
          className={cn(
            'h-5 w-5 shrink-0 rounded-md border flex items-center justify-center',
            'transition-all duration-fast ease-in-out',
            'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background',
            checked
              ? 'border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-xs'
              : 'border-input bg-card group-hover:border-primary/80 group-hover:bg-accent/40'
          )}
        >
          {checked ? (
            <Check aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 stroke-[3] text-primary-foreground" />
          ) : null}
        </button>
        {label ? (
          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground group-hover:text-foreground/90">
            {label}
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </label>
    );
  }
);

Checkbox.displayName = 'Checkbox';

# FILE: src\components\primitives\Input\Input.tsx
================================================================================
import * as React from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';
import { inputVariants, type InputVariantProps } from '@/design-system/variants/input.variants';

export interface InputProps
  extends Omit<React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, 'size'>,
    InputVariantProps {
  error?: string;
  label?: string;
  description?: string;
  leftIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  rightIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  containerClassName?: string;
}

export const Input = React.forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  (
    {
      className,
      containerClassName,
      variant = 'default',
      size = 'md',
      type = 'text',
      error,
      label,
      description,
      leftIcon,
      rightIcon,
      id,
      ...props
    },
    ref
  ) => {
    const generatedId = React.useId();
    const inputId = id ?? generatedId;
    const descriptionId = `${inputId}-description`;
    const errorId = `${inputId}-error`;
    const hasError = Boolean(error);
    const resolvedVariant = hasError ? 'error' : variant;

    return (
      <div className={cn('w-full space-y-1.5', containerClassName)}>
        {label ? (
          <label
            htmlFor={inputId}
            className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground peer-disabled:cursor-not-allowed peer-disabled:opacity-70"
          >
            {label}
          </label>
        ) : null}
        <div className="relative">
          {leftIcon ? (
            <span
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="pointer-events-none absolute start-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground"
            >
              {leftIcon}
            </span>
          ) : null}
          <input
            ref={ref}
            id={inputId}
            type={type}
            aria-invalid={hasError || undefined}
            aria-describedby={
              hasError ? errorId : description ? descriptionId : undefined
            }
            className={cn(
              inputVariants({ variant: resolvedVariant, size }),
              leftIcon && 'ps-10',
              rightIcon && 'pe-10',
              className
            )}
            {...props}
          />
          {rightIcon ? (
            <span
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="pointer-events-none absolute end-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground"
            >
              {rightIcon}
            </span>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        {hasError ? (
          <p id={errorId} role="alert" className="text-base text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        ) : description ? (
          <p id={descriptionId} className="text-base text-muted-foreground">
            {description}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);

Input.displayName = 'Input';

import * as React from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

export interface SkeletonProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> {
  variant?: 'rect' | 'circle' | 'text';
}

const variantClasses = {
  rect: 'rounded-xl',
  circle: 'rounded-full',
  text: 'rounded-md h-4 w-full',
} as const;

export const Skeleton = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, SkeletonProps>(
  ({ className, variant = 'rect', ...props }, ref) => (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={cn('bg-muted/70', variantClasses[variant], className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
);

Skeleton.displayName = 'Skeleton';

// src/components/primitives/Switch/Switch.tsx
'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

export interface SwitchProps {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
  className?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  id?: string;
  'aria-label'?: string;
}

export const Switch = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, SwitchProps>(
  ({ checked, onChange, className, disabled = false, id, 'aria-label': ariaLabel }, ref) => {
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        id={id}
        type="button"
        role="switch"
        aria-checked={checked}
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        disabled={disabled}
        onClick={() => !disabled && onChange(!checked)}
        className={cn(
          'relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-full p-0.5',
          'transition-colors duration-200 ease-in-out',
          'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background',
          'disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50',
          // استفاده از Flex جهت تراز کردن کاملاً مستقل از راست‌به‌چپ یا چپ‌به‌راست بودن
          checked ? 'justify-start bg-primary' : 'justify-end bg-input',
          className
        )}
      >
        <motion.span
          layout
          transition={{
            type: 'spring',
            stiffness: 600,
            damping: 35,
          }}
          className="pointer-events-none block h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-white shadow-md"
        />
      </button>
    );
  }
);

Switch.displayName = 'Switch';

================================================================================
# FILE: src\components\primitives\Typography\Typography.tsx
================================================================================
import * as React from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';
import {
  typographyVariants,
  type TypographyVariantProps,
} from '@/design-system/variants/typography.variants';

type VariantKey = NonNullable<TypographyVariantProps['variant']>;

const defaultElement: Record<VariantKey, React.ElementType> = {
  h1: 'h1',
  h2: 'h2',
  h3: 'h3',
  h4: 'h4',
  h5: 'h5',
  h6: 'h6',
  p: 'p',
  lead: 'p',
  large: 'div',
  small: 'span',
  muted: 'p',
  code: 'code',
  blockquote: 'blockquote',
};

export interface TypographyProps
  extends Omit<React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>, 'color'>,
    TypographyVariantProps {
  as?: React.ElementType;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const Typography = React.forwardRef<HTMLElement, TypographyProps>(
  (
    {
      className,
      variant = 'p',
      align,
      color = 'default',
      weight,
      as,
      children,
      ...props
    },
    ref
  ) => {
    const Component = (as ?? defaultElement[variant as VariantKey] ?? 'p') as React.ElementType;
    return (
      <Component
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          typographyVariants({ variant, align, color, weight }),
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </Component>
    );
  }
);

Typography.displayName = 'Typography';

export const H1 = React.forwardRef<HTMLHeadingElement, Omit<TypographyProps, 'variant' | 'as'>>(
  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="h1" variant="h1" {...props} />
);
H1.displayName = 'H1';

export const H2 = React.forwardRef<HTMLHeadingElement, Omit<TypographyProps, 'variant' | 'as'>>(
  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="h2" variant="h2" {...props} />
);
H2.displayName = 'H2';

export const H3 = React.forwardRef<HTMLHeadingElement, Omit<TypographyProps, 'variant' | 'as'>>(
  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="h3" variant="h3" {...props} />
);
H3.displayName = 'H3';

export const H4 = React.forwardRef<HTMLHeadingElement, Omit<TypographyProps, 'variant' | 'as'>>(
  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="h4" variant="h4" {...props} />
);
H4.displayName = 'H4';

export const P = React.forwardRef<HTMLParagraphElement, Omit<TypographyProps, 'variant' | 'as'>>(
  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="p" variant="p" {...props} />
);
P.displayName = 'P';

export const Lead = React.forwardRef<HTMLParagraphElement, Omit<TypographyProps, 'variant' | 'as'>>(
  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="p" variant="lead" {...props} />
);
Lead.displayName = 'Lead';

export const Large = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, Omit<TypographyProps, 'variant' | 'as'>>(
  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="div" variant="large" {...props} />
);
Large.displayName = 'Large';

export const Small = React.forwardRef<HTMLSpanElement, Omit<TypographyProps, 'variant' | 'as'>>(
  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="span" variant="small" {...props} />
);
Small.displayName = 'Small';

  (props, ref) => <Typography ref={ref} as="p" variant="muted" {...props} />
);
Muted.displayName = 'Muted';

# FILE: src\core\errors\api-error.ts

// src/core/errors/api-error.ts

import { ErrorType, ErrorSeverity, ErrorDetail } from './types/error.types';

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly type: ErrorType;
  readonly status?: number;
  readonly severity: ErrorSeverity;
  readonly details?: ErrorDetail[];
  readonly userMessage: string;
  readonly technicalMessage?: string;
  readonly timestamp: string;
  readonly originalError?: unknown;

  constructor(config: {
    type: ErrorType;
    status?: number;
    message: string;
    userMessage?: string;
    technicalMessage?: string;
    details?: ErrorDetail[];
    severity?: ErrorSeverity;
    originalError?: unknown;
  }) {
    super(config.message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    
    this.type = config.type;
    this.status = config.status;
    this.severity = config.severity || this.determineSeverity(config.type);
    this.details = config.details;
    this.userMessage = config.userMessage || this.getDefaultUserMessage(config.type);
    this.technicalMessage = config.technicalMessage;
    this.timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    this.originalError = config.originalError;

    if (Error.captureStackTrace) {
      Error.captureStackTrace(this, ApiError);
    }
  }

  private determineSeverity(type: ErrorType): ErrorSeverity {
    const severityMap: Record<ErrorType, ErrorSeverity> = {
      [ErrorType.VALIDATION]: ErrorSeverity.LOW,
      [ErrorType.BAD_REQUEST]: ErrorSeverity.LOW,
      [ErrorType.UNAUTHORIZED]: ErrorSeverity.MEDIUM,
      [ErrorType.FORBIDDEN]: ErrorSeverity.HIGH,
      [ErrorType.NOT_FOUND]: ErrorSeverity.MEDIUM,
      [ErrorType.CONFLICT]: ErrorSeverity.MEDIUM,
      [ErrorType.RATE_LIMIT]: ErrorSeverity.MEDIUM,
      [ErrorType.SERVER_ERROR]: ErrorSeverity.HIGH,
      [ErrorType.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE]: ErrorSeverity.HIGH,
      [ErrorType.NETWORK]: ErrorSeverity.MEDIUM,
      [ErrorType.TIMEOUT]: ErrorSeverity.MEDIUM,
      [ErrorType.ABORTED]: ErrorSeverity.LOW,
      [ErrorType.BUSINESS]: ErrorSeverity.MEDIUM,
      [ErrorType.PERMISSION]: ErrorSeverity.HIGH,
      [ErrorType.UNKNOWN]: ErrorSeverity.CRITICAL,
    };

    return severityMap[type] || ErrorSeverity.MEDIUM;
  }

  private getDefaultUserMessage(type: ErrorType): string {
    const messages: Record<ErrorType, string> = {
      [ErrorType.VALIDATION]: 'لطفاً اطلاعات وارد شده را بررسی کنید.',
      [ErrorType.BAD_REQUEST]: 'درخواست نامعتبر است.',
      [ErrorType.UNAUTHORIZED]: 'لطفاً مجدداً وارد حساب کاربری خود شوید.',
      [ErrorType.FORBIDDEN]: 'شما دسترسی لازم برای این عملیات را ندارید.',
      [ErrorType.NOT_FOUND]: 'اطلاعات مورد نظر یافت نشد.',
      [ErrorType.CONFLICT]: 'اطلاعات تکراری است.',
      [ErrorType.RATE_LIMIT]: 'تعداد درخواست‌های شما بیش از حد مجاز است. لطفاً چند لحظه صبر کنید.',
      [ErrorType.SERVER_ERROR]: 'خطایی در سرور رخ داده است. لطفاً دوباره تلاش کنید.',
      [ErrorType.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE]: 'سرویس در حال حاضر در دسترس نیست.',
      [ErrorType.NETWORK]: 'ارتباط با سرور برقرار نشد. لطفاً اتصال اینترنت خود را بررسی کنید.',
      [ErrorType.TIMEOUT]: 'درخواست شما زمان‌بر بود. لطفاً دوباره تلاش کنید.',
      [ErrorType.ABORTED]: 'درخواست لغو شد.',
      [ErrorType.BUSINESS]: 'عملیات با شکست مواجه شد.',
      [ErrorType.PERMISSION]: 'شما مجوز انجام این عملیات را ندارید.',
      [ErrorType.UNKNOWN]: 'خطای ناشناخته‌ای رخ داده است.',
    };

    return messages[type] || 'خطایی رخ داده است. لطفاً دوباره تلاش کنید.';
  }

  isClientError(): boolean {
    return this.status !== undefined && this.status >= 400 && this.status < 500;
  }

  isServerError(): boolean {
    return this.status !== undefined && this.status >= 500;
  }

  isNetworkError(): boolean {
    return [ErrorType.NETWORK, ErrorType.TIMEOUT, ErrorType.ABORTED].includes(this.type);
  }

  isAuthenticationError(): boolean {
    return [ErrorType.UNAUTHORIZED, ErrorType.FORBIDDEN].includes(this.type);
  }

  getFieldErrors(): Record<string, string> {
    if (!this.details || this.details.length === 0) {
      return {};
    }

    return this.details.reduce((acc, detail) => {
      if (detail.field) {
        acc[detail.field] = detail.message;
      }
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<string, string>);
  }

  toJSON(): Record<string, unknown> {
    return {
      name: this.name,
      type: this.type,
      status: this.status,
      message: this.message,
      userMessage: this.userMessage,
      technicalMessage: this.technicalMessage,
      details: this.details,
      severity: this.severity,
      timestamp: this.timestamp,
    };
  }
}

================================================================================
# FILE: src\core\errors\error-manager.ts
================================================================================
import { ApiError } from './api-error';
import { ErrorType } from './types/error.types';
import { ErrorNormalizer } from './error-normalizer';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';
import { showToast } from '../utils/toast';

export interface ErrorHandler {
  canHandle(error: ApiError): boolean;
  handle(error: ApiError): void;
  getPriority(): number;
}

export class ErrorManager {
  private static instance: ErrorManager;
  private handlers: ErrorHandler[] = [];

  private constructor() {
    this.registerDefaultHandlers();
  }

  static getInstance(): ErrorManager {
    if (!this.instance) {
      this.instance = new ErrorManager();
    }
    return this.instance;
  }

  private registerDefaultHandlers(): void {
    // ۱. هندلر متمرکز و با اولویت بالای خطاهای سروری ۵۰۲ و ۵۰۳ (هدایت امن به صفحه بروزرسانی بدون تخریب سشن کاربر)
    this.registerHandler({
      canHandle: (error) => 
        error.status === 502 || 
        error.status === 503 || 
        error.type === ErrorType.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
      handle: (error) => {
        logger.error('[ErrorManager] Server maintenance / Bad Gateway detected:', error.message);
        
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
          // انتقال امن به صفحه بروزرسانی بدون تغییر کوکی‌ها یا از بین بردن حالت ورود کاربر
          if (currentPath !== '/maintenance') {
            window.location.href = '/maintenance';
          }
        }
      },
      getPriority: () => 11, // بالاترین اولویت برای خطاهای حیاتی سرور
    });

    this.registerHandler({
      canHandle: (error) => error.type === ErrorType.VALIDATION,
      handle: (error) => {
        logger.debug('Validation error:', error.getFieldErrors());
        this.triggerToastIfNeeded(error);
      },
      getPriority: () => 10,
    });

    this.registerHandler({
      canHandle: (error) => error.type === ErrorType.UNAUTHORIZED,
      handle: (error) => {
        logger.warn('Unauthorized access:', error.message);
        
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
          const originalError = error.originalError as any;
          const requestUrl = originalError?.config?.url || '';

          const isPassiveAuthCheck = 
            requestUrl.includes('/User') || 
            requestUrl.includes('/Refresh') || 
            requestUrl.includes('/get-user');

          if (currentPath !== '/login' && !isPassiveAuthCheck && currentPath !== '/maintenance') {
            window.location.href = `/login?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(currentPath)}`;
          }
        }
      },
      getPriority: () => 9,
    });

    this.registerHandler({
      canHandle: (error) => error.type === ErrorType.FORBIDDEN,
      handle: (error) => {
        logger.warn('Forbidden access:', error.message);
      },
      getPriority: () => 8,
    });

    this.registerHandler({
      canHandle: (error) => error.type === ErrorType.RATE_LIMIT,
      handle: (error) => {
        logger.warn('Rate limit exceeded:', error.message);
        this.triggerToastIfNeeded(error);
      },
      getPriority: () => 7,
    });

    this.registerHandler({
      canHandle: (error) => error.isNetworkError(),
      handle: (error) => {
        logger.error('Network error:', error.message);
        this.triggerToastIfNeeded(error);
      },
      getPriority: () => 6,
    });

    this.registerHandler({
      canHandle: (error) => error.type === ErrorType.NOT_FOUND,
      handle: (error) => {
        logger.warn('Not found:', error.message);
      },
      getPriority: () => 5,
    });

    this.registerHandler({
      canHandle: () => true,
      handle: (error) => {
        logger.error('Unhandled error:', {
          type: error.type,
          status: error.status,
          message: error.message,
          userMessage: error.userMessage,
          details: error.details,
        });
        this.triggerToastIfNeeded(error);
      },
      getPriority: () => 0,
    });
  }

  private triggerToastIfNeeded(error: ApiError): void {
    const originalError = error.originalError as any;
    const method = originalError?.config?.method?.toLowerCase();
    const isMutation = method && method !== 'get';

    if (isMutation && error.type !== ErrorType.ABORTED) {
      showToast.error(error.userMessage);
    }
  }

  registerHandler(handler: ErrorHandler): void {
    this.handlers.push(handler);
    this.handlers.sort((a, b) => b.getPriority() - a.getPriority());
  }

  handleError(error: unknown): void {
    const apiError = this.normalize(error);
    
    const handler = this.handlers.find(h => h.canHandle(apiError));
    
    if (handler) {
      handler.handle(apiError);
    } else {
      logger.error('No handler found for error:', apiError);
    }
  }

  normalize(error: unknown): ApiError {
    return ErrorNormalizer.normalize(error);
  }
}

export const errorManager = ErrorManager.getInstance();

================================================================================
# FILE: src\core\errors\error-normalizer.ts
================================================================================
// src/core/errors/error-normalizer.ts

import axios, { AxiosError } from 'axios';
import { ApiError } from './api-error';
import { ErrorType, ErrorDetail } from './types/error.types';

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*                                   Types                                    */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

interface NormalizedErrorDetails {
  message: string;
  userMessage?: string;
  details?: ErrorDetail[];
}

interface ApiErrorLikeResponse {
  status: number;
  message?: unknown;
  error?: unknown;
  errors?: unknown;
  userMessage?: unknown;
  user_message?: unknown;
  errorMessage?: unknown;
  error_message?: unknown;
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*                                 Constants                                  */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

const DEFAULT_USER_MESSAGE = 'خطایی رخ داده است.';

const STATUS_TO_USER_MESSAGE: Record<number, string> = {
  400: 'اطلاعات وارد شده صحیح نیست.',
  401: 'لطفاً مجدداً وارد حساب کاربری خود شوید.',
  403: 'شما دسترسی لازم را ندارید.',
  404: 'اطلاعات مورد نظر یافت نشد.',
  409: 'اطلاعات تکراری است.',
  422: 'لطفاً اطلاعات را بررسی کنید.',
  429: 'تعداد درخواست‌ها بیش از حد مجاز است.',
  500: 'خطای سرور. لطفاً دوباره تلاش کنید.',
  503: 'سرویس در دسترس نیست.',
};

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*                                  Helper                                    */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

function looksLikeHtml(value: string): boolean {
  const trimmed = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  return (
    trimmed.startsWith('<!doctype') ||
    trimmed.startsWith('<html') ||
    trimmed.startsWith('<body') ||
    /<\/?[a-z][\s\S]*>/i.test(trimmed)
  );
}

/**
 * ساخت ApiError به‌صورت امن.
 * اگر سازنده ApiError به هر دلیلی throw کند، یک ApiError ساده برمی‌گرداند.
 */
function safeCreateApiError(params: {
  type: ErrorType;
  status?: number;
  message: string;
  userMessage?: string;
  details?: ErrorDetail[];
  originalError: unknown;
}): ApiError {
  const message = params.message?.trim() || 'Unknown error';
  const userMessage = params.userMessage?.trim() || DEFAULT_USER_MESSAGE;

  try {
    return new ApiError({
      type: params.type,
      status: params.status,
      message,
      userMessage,
      details: params.details,
      originalError: params.originalError,
    });
  } catch {
    try {
      return new ApiError({
        type: ErrorType.UNKNOWN,
        status: params.status,
        message: 'Unknown error',
        userMessage: DEFAULT_USER_MESSAGE,
        originalError: params.originalError,
      });
    } catch {
      // 🚨 آخرین سنگر: اگر حتی ApiError با ورودی حداقلی هم ساخت نشد،
      // یک Error استاندارد برمی‌گردانیم (به عنوان fallback نهایی)
      const err = new Error(message) as Error & {
        type?: ErrorType;
        status?: number;
        userMessage?: string;
        originalError?: unknown;
      };
      err.type = ErrorType.UNKNOWN;
      err.status = params.status;
      err.userMessage = DEFAULT_USER_MESSAGE;
      err.originalError = params.originalError;
      return err as unknown as ApiError;
    }
  }
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*                              ErrorNormalizer                               */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

export class ErrorNormalizer {
  static normalize(error: unknown): ApiError {
    if (error instanceof ApiError) {
      return error;
    }

    if (axios.isAxiosError(error)) {
      return this.normalizeAxiosError(error);
    }

    if (this.isApiErrorResponse(error)) {
      return this.normalizeApiResponse(error);
    }

    if (this.isAbortError(error)) {
      return safeCreateApiError({
        type: ErrorType.ABORTED,
        message: 'Request aborted',
        userMessage: 'درخواست لغو شد.',
        originalError: error,
      });
    }

    if (this.isErrorObject(error)) {
      return safeCreateApiError({
        type: ErrorType.UNKNOWN,
        message: error.message || 'Unknown error',
        userMessage: 'خطای ناشناخته‌ای رخ داده است.',
        originalError: error,
      });
    }

    return safeCreateApiError({
      type: ErrorType.UNKNOWN,
      message: 'Unknown error occurred',
      userMessage: 'خطای ناشناخته‌ای رخ داده است.',
      originalError: error,
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /*                              Type Guards                                 */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  private static isErrorObject(error: unknown): error is Error {
    return (
      typeof error === 'object' &&
      error !== null &&
      'message' in error &&
      typeof (error as { message: unknown }).message === 'string'
    );
  }

  private static isAbortError(error: unknown): boolean {
    return (
      typeof error === 'object' &&
      error !== null &&
      'name' in error &&
      (error as { name: unknown }).name === 'AbortError'
    );
  }

  private static isApiErrorResponse(error: unknown): error is ApiErrorLikeResponse {
    return (
      typeof error === 'object' &&
      error !== null &&
      'status' in error &&
      typeof (error as { status: unknown }).status === 'number' &&
      Number.isFinite((error as { status: number }).status)
    );
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /*                            Axios Normalizer                              */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  private static normalizeAxiosError(error: AxiosError): ApiError {
    if (error.code === 'ERR_NETWORK') {
      return safeCreateApiError({
        type: ErrorType.NETWORK,
        message: error.message,
        userMessage: 'ارتباط با سرور برقرار نشد. اتصال اینترنت خود را بررسی کنید.',
        originalError: error,
      });
    }

    if (error.code === 'ECONNABORTED' || error.message.includes('timeout')) {
      return safeCreateApiError({
        type: ErrorType.TIMEOUT,
        message: error.message,
        userMessage: 'درخواست زمان‌بر بود. مجدداً تلاش کنید.',
        originalError: error,
      });
    }

    if (error.response) {
      const status = error.response.status;
      const rawData: unknown = error.response.data;

      if (typeof rawData === 'string' && looksLikeHtml(rawData)) {
        return safeCreateApiError({
          type: this.getErrorTypeFromStatus(status),
          status,
          message: `HTTP ${status} returned HTML instead of JSON`,
          userMessage:
            STATUS_TO_USER_MESSAGE[status] ??
            'سرویس در دسترس نیست. لطفاً دوباره تلاش کنید.',
          originalError: error,
        });
      }

      const errorDetails = this.extractErrorDetails(rawData);

      return safeCreateApiError({
        type: this.getErrorTypeFromStatus(status),
        status,
        message: errorDetails?.message || error.message,
        userMessage:
          errorDetails?.userMessage ??
          STATUS_TO_USER_MESSAGE[status] ??
          DEFAULT_USER_MESSAGE,
        details: errorDetails?.details,
        originalError: error,
      });
    }

    return safeCreateApiError({
      type: ErrorType.UNKNOWN,
      message: error.message,
      userMessage: 'خطایی در ارسال درخواست رخ داده است.',
      originalError: error,
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /*                          API Response Normalizer                         */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  private static normalizeApiResponse(error: ApiErrorLikeResponse): ApiError {
    const status = Number.isFinite(error.status) ? error.status : 500;
    const type = this.getErrorTypeFromStatus(status);

    const details: ErrorDetail[] = [];
    if (Array.isArray(error.errors)) {
      for (const item of error.errors) {
        if (typeof item === 'string') {
          details.push({ message: item });
          continue;
        }
        if (typeof item === 'object' && item !== null) {
          const obj = item as Record<string, unknown>;
          details.push({
            field:
              typeof obj.field === 'string'
                ? obj.field
                : typeof obj.property === 'string'
                  ? obj.property
                  : undefined,
            message:
              typeof obj.message === 'string'
                ? obj.message
                : typeof obj.error === 'string'
                  ? obj.error
                  : 'Unknown error',
            code: typeof obj.code === 'string' ? obj.code : undefined,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    const rawMessage =
      (typeof error.message === 'string' && error.message) ||
      (typeof error.error === 'string' && error.error) ||
      'Unknown error';

    return safeCreateApiError({
      type,
      status,
      message: rawMessage,
      userMessage: this.getUserMessageFromError(error, status),
      details: details.length > 0 ? details : undefined,
      originalError: error,
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /*                                 Helpers                                  */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  private static getErrorTypeFromStatus(status: number): ErrorType {
    const statusMap: Record<number, ErrorType> = {
      400: ErrorType.BAD_REQUEST,
      401: ErrorType.UNAUTHORIZED,
      403: ErrorType.FORBIDDEN,
      404: ErrorType.NOT_FOUND,
      409: ErrorType.CONFLICT,
      422: ErrorType.VALIDATION,
      429: ErrorType.RATE_LIMIT,
      500: ErrorType.SERVER_ERROR,
      503: ErrorType.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
    };

    return statusMap[status] || ErrorType.UNKNOWN;
  }

  private static extractErrorDetails(data: unknown): NormalizedErrorDetails | null {
    if (data === null || data === undefined) return null;

    if (typeof data === 'string') {
      const trimmed = data.trim();
      if (!trimmed) return null;

      if (looksLikeHtml(trimmed)) {
        const stripped = trimmed
          .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, ' ')
          .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
          .trim();
        return {
          message: stripped.slice(0, 200) || 'Server error',
          userMessage: 'سرویس در دسترس نیست.',
        };
      }

      return { message: trimmed, userMessage: trimmed };
    }

    if (typeof data !== 'object') return null;

    const obj = data as Record<string, unknown>;

    if (
      obj.errors &&
      typeof obj.errors === 'object' &&
      !Array.isArray(obj.errors)
    ) {
      const errorKeys = Object.keys(obj.errors as Record<string, unknown>);
      if (errorKeys.length > 0) {
        const firstKey = errorKeys[0];
        const list = (obj.errors as Record<string, unknown>)[firstKey];

        if (Array.isArray(list) && list.length > 0) {
          const persianMsg =
            list.find(
              (msg): msg is string =>
                typeof msg === 'string' && /^[\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(msg),
            ) ?? (typeof list[0] === 'string' ? list[0] : undefined);

          if (persianMsg) {
            return { message: persianMsg, userMessage: persianMsg };
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const rawMessage =
      (typeof obj.message === 'string' && obj.message) ||
      (typeof obj.error === 'string' && obj.error) ||
      (typeof obj.errorMessage === 'string' && obj.errorMessage) ||
      (typeof obj.error_message === 'string' && obj.error_message) ||
      (typeof obj.userMessage === 'string' && obj.userMessage) ||
      (typeof obj.user_message === 'string' && obj.user_message) ||
      (typeof obj.errors === 'string' && obj.errors) ||
      '';

    if (rawMessage) {
      return {
        message: rawMessage,
        userMessage: rawMessage,
        details: Array.isArray(obj.errors)
          ? (obj.errors as ErrorDetail[])
          : undefined,
      };
    }

    return null;
  }

  private static getUserMessageFromError(
    error: ApiErrorLikeResponse,
    status: number,
  ): string {
    if (typeof error.userMessage === 'string' && error.userMessage.trim()) {
      return error.userMessage;
    }
    if (typeof error.user_message === 'string' && error.user_message.trim()) {
      return error.user_message;
    }

    return STATUS_TO_USER_MESSAGE[status] || DEFAULT_USER_MESSAGE;
  }
}

// src/core/errors/types/error.types.ts

export enum ErrorType {
  // Client Errors (4xx)
  BAD_REQUEST = 'bad_request',
  UNAUTHORIZED = 'unauthorized',
  FORBIDDEN = 'forbidden',
  NOT_FOUND = 'not_found',
  CONFLICT = 'conflict',
  VALIDATION = 'validation',
  RATE_LIMIT = 'rate_limit',

  // Server Errors (5xx)
  SERVER_ERROR = 'server_error',
  SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE = 'service_unavailable',

  // Network Errors
  NETWORK = 'network',
  TIMEOUT = 'timeout',
  ABORTED = 'aborted',

  // Business Logic Errors
  BUSINESS = 'business',
  PERMISSION = 'permission',

  // Unknown
  UNKNOWN = 'unknown',
}

export enum ErrorSeverity {
  LOW = 'low',
  MEDIUM = 'medium',
  HIGH = 'high',
  CRITICAL = 'critical',
}

export interface ErrorDetail {
  field?: string;
  message: string;
  code?: string;
  value?: unknown;
}

// src/core/http/client.ts

import axios, { AxiosInstance, AxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';
import { env } from '../config/env';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';
import { errorManager } from '../errors/error-manager';
import { ApiError } from '../errors/api-error';
import { HttpResponse, RequestOptions, HttpMethod } from './types';
import http from 'http';
import https from 'https';

const keepAliveHttpAgent = typeof window === 'undefined'
  ? new http.Agent({ keepAlive: true, maxSockets: 100, maxFreeSockets: 10, timeout: 60000 })
  : null;

const keepAliveHttpsAgent = typeof window === 'undefined'
  ? new https.Agent({ keepAlive: true, maxSockets: 100, maxFreeSockets: 10, timeout: 60000 })
  : null;

// جلوگیری از حملات Open Redirect: مسیر فقط باید نسبی و داخلی باشد
function getSafeRedirectUrl(targetPath: string): string {
  if (!targetPath || !targetPath.startsWith('/') || targetPath.startsWith('//') || targetPath.includes('\\')) {
    return '/profile';
  }
  return targetPath;
}

// سانسور دیتای حساس قبل از ثبت در لاگ‌ها (جلوگیری از لو رفتن OTP و شماره کاربر)
function sanitizeLogData(data: any): any {
  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return data;
  const sanitized = { ...data };
  const sensitiveKeys = ['code', 'password', 'token', 'refreshToken', 'nationalCode'];
  for (const key of Object.keys(sanitized)) {
    if (sensitiveKeys.includes(key)) {
      sanitized[key] = '***REDACTED***';
    }
  }
  return sanitized;
}

export class HttpClient {
  private readonly axiosInstance: AxiosInstance;
  private abortControllers: Map<string, AbortController> = new Map();
  private serverRequestConfig: Record<string, any> = {};
  private isRefreshing = false;
  private failedQueue: { resolve: (value: any) => void; reject: (reason: any) => void }[] = [];

  constructor() {
    const baseURL = typeof window === 'undefined'
      ? (env.apiBaseUrl || 'https://api.yadakchi.local')
      : '/proxy-api';
    
    this.axiosInstance = axios.create({
      baseURL,
      timeout: env.apiTimeout,
      withCredentials: true, // استفاده ایمن از HttpOnly Cookies
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Accept': 'application/json',
        'X-App-Version': env.appVersion,
      },
      httpAgent: keepAliveHttpAgent,
      httpsAgent: keepAliveHttpsAgent,
    });

    this.setupInterceptors();
  }

  setServerConfig(config: Record<string, any>) {
    this.serverRequestConfig = config;
  }

  private setupInterceptors(): void {
    this.axiosInstance.interceptors.request.use(
      (config) => {
        if (typeof window === 'undefined' && this.serverRequestConfig?.headers) {
          config.headers = {
            ...config.headers,
            ...this.serverRequestConfig.headers,
          };
        }

        if (env.enableLogging && env.isDevelopment) {
          logger.debug(`[HTTP] ${config.method?.toUpperCase()} ${config.baseURL}${config.url}`, {
            params: config.params,
            data: sanitizeLogData(config.data),
          });
        }

        const requestId = this.generateRequestId();
        config.headers['X-Request-ID'] = requestId;

        return config;
      },
      (error) => {
        logger.error('[HTTP] Request error', error);
        return Promise.reject(error);
      }
    );

    this.axiosInstance.interceptors.response.use(
      (response) => {
        if (env.enableLogging && env.isDevelopment) {
          logger.debug(`[HTTP] Response ${response.status} ${response.config.url}`, {
            status: response.status,
          });
        }
        return response;
      },
      async (error) => {
        const originalRequest = error.config;

        if (error.response?.status === 401 && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
          const requestUrl = originalRequest.url || '';

          if (currentPath === '/login') {
            return Promise.reject(errorManager.normalize(error));
          }

          const isPassiveAuthCheck = 
            requestUrl.includes('/User') || 
            requestUrl.includes('/Refresh') || 
            requestUrl.includes('/get-user') ||
            requestUrl.includes('/GetFrontBasket') ||
            requestUrl.includes('/IsUserFavoriteProduct') ||
            requestUrl.includes('/UserVehicle');

          if (isPassiveAuthCheck) {
            return Promise.reject(errorManager.normalize(error));
          }

          const safeRedirect = getSafeRedirectUrl(currentPath);

          if (originalRequest._retry) {
            window.location.href = `/login?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(safeRedirect)}`;
            return Promise.reject(errorManager.normalize(error));
          }

          if (this.isRefreshing) {
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
              this.failedQueue.push({ resolve, reject });
            })
              .then(() => this.axiosInstance(originalRequest))
              .catch((err) => Promise.reject(err));
          }

          originalRequest._retry = true;
          this.isRefreshing = true;

          return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            this.axiosInstance.post('/api/Login/Refresh')
              .then(() => {
                this.failedQueue.forEach((prom) => prom.resolve(null));
                this.failedQueue = [];
                resolve(this.axiosInstance(originalRequest));
              })
              .catch((refreshError) => {
                this.failedQueue.forEach((prom) => prom.reject(refreshError));
                this.failedQueue = [];
                window.location.href = `/login?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(safeRedirect)}`;
                reject(refreshError);
              })
              .finally(() => {
                this.isRefreshing = false;
              });
          });
        }

        const apiError = errorManager.normalize(error);
        return Promise.reject(apiError);
      }
    );
  }

  private generateRequestId(): string {
    return `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;
  }

  private async makeRequest<T = unknown>(
    method: HttpMethod,
    url: string,
    data?: unknown,
    options?: RequestOptions
  ): Promise<HttpResponse<T>> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const requestId = this.generateRequestId();

    let signal = options?.signal;
    let controller: AbortController | undefined;

    if (!signal) {
      controller = new AbortController();
      signal = controller.signal;
      this.abortControllers.set(requestId, controller);
    }

    try {
      let requestBaseURL = this.axiosInstance.defaults.baseURL;
      if (url.startsWith('/api/Search') && typeof window === 'undefined') {
        requestBaseURL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SEARCH_API_BASE_URL || 'http://172.92.92.237:7104';
      }

      const config: AxiosRequestConfig = {
        method,
        url,
        baseURL: requestBaseURL,
        params: options?.params,
        headers: options?.headers,
        timeout: options?.timeout ?? env.apiTimeout,
        signal,
        withCredentials: true, 
      };

      if (data !== undefined) {
        config.data = data;
      }

      const response = await this.axiosInstance.request<T>(config);

      if (controller) {
        this.abortControllers.delete(requestId);
      }

      const duration = Date.now() - startTime;

      return {
        data: response.data,
        status: response.status,
        statusText: response.statusText,
        headers: response.headers as Record<string, string>,
        config: {
          url: response.config.url || '',
          method: response.config.method as HttpMethod,
          data: response.config.data,
          params: response.config.params,
          headers: response.config.headers as Record<string, string>,
        },
        duration,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      if (controller) {
        this.abortControllers.delete(requestId);
      }

      if (error instanceof ApiError) {
        throw error;
      }

      const apiError = errorManager.normalize(error);
      throw apiError;
    }
  }

  async get<T = unknown>(url: string, options?: RequestOptions): Promise<HttpResponse<T>> {
    return this.makeRequest<T>('GET', url, undefined, options);
  }

  async post<T = unknown>(
    url: string,
    data?: unknown,
    options?: RequestOptions
  ): Promise<HttpResponse<T>> {
    return this.makeRequest<T>('POST', url, data, options);
  }

  async put<T = unknown>(
    url: string,
    data?: unknown,
    options?: RequestOptions
  ): Promise<HttpResponse<T>> {
    return this.makeRequest<T>('PUT', url, data, options);
  }

  async delete<T = unknown>(url: string, data?: unknown, options?: RequestOptions): Promise<HttpResponse<T>> {
    return this.makeRequest<T>('DELETE', url, data, options);
  }

  async cancelRequest(requestId: string): Promise<void> {
    const controller = this.abortControllers.get(requestId);
    if (controller) {
      controller.abort();
      this.abortControllers.delete(requestId);
    }
  }

  cancelAllRequests(): void {
    this.abortControllers.forEach((controller) => controller.abort());
    this.abortControllers.clear();
  }

  setBaseURL(url: string): void {
    this.axiosInstance.defaults.baseURL = url;
  }

  setDefaultTimeout(timeout: number): void {
    this.axiosInstance.defaults.timeout = timeout;
  }
}

let httpClientInstance: HttpClient | null = null;

export function getHttpClient(): HttpClient {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return new HttpClient();
  }
  if (!httpClientInstance) {
    httpClientInstance = new HttpClient();
  }
  return httpClientInstance;
}

// src/core/http/types/index.ts

export type HttpMethod = 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE' | 'PATCH';

export interface RequestOptions {
  params?: Record<string, unknown>;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  timeout?: number;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  retry?: RetryOptions;
}

export interface RetryOptions {
  maxRetries: number;
  delay: number;
  shouldRetry?: (error: unknown) => boolean;
}

export interface HttpResponse<T = unknown> {
  data: T;
  status: number;
  statusText: string;
  headers: Record<string, string>;
  config: HttpRequest;
  duration: number;
}

export interface HttpRequest {
  url: string;
  method: HttpMethod;
  data?: unknown;
  params?: Record<string, unknown>;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
}

export interface PaginatedRequest extends RequestOptions {
  pageNumber?: number;
  pageSize?: number;
}

export interface ApiErrorResponse {
  status: number;
  message: string;
  errors?: Array<{
    field?: string;
    message: string;
    code?: string;
  }>;
  timestamp?: string;
  path?: string;
}
